  ];

  // Exchange with the central kg-microbe knowledge graph.
  var HUB = [
    { mech: "CultureMech", dir: "out", dataExchange: false, summary: "Shared record formats; no published data transfer established by this snapshot.", kind: "namespace", what: "shares the kg-microbe mech-shared schema modules; its SSSOM and KGX exporters have not published a release yet" },
    { mech: "MediaIngredientMech", dir: "out", dataExchange: true, summary: "Reviewed ingredient names and chemical identities are available as candidate links to kg-microbe.", kind: "export", what: "reviewed ingredient mappings staged for candidate integration; production promotion remains pending" },
    { mech: "CommunityMech", dir: "out", dataExchange: false, summary: "Shared record formats; no published data transfer established by this snapshot.", kind: "namespace", what: "shares the kg-microbe mech-shared schema modules; its KGX exporter and release workflow have not published a release yet" },
    { mech: "TraitMech", dir: "in", dataExchange: true, summary: "Connections in kg-microbe help TraitMech show matching and neighboring microbial traits.", kind: "input", what: "DeepWalk graph embeddings of kg-microbe's trait nodes, vendored for the kg-microbe matches and nearest neighbors on the trait pages" },
    { mech: "TaxonMech", dir: "in", dataExchange: true, summary: "kg-microbe supplies microbial names, classifications and reports about organisms, their growth media and traits.", kind: "input", what: "GTDB mappings, LPSN nomenclature and MediaDive, GOLD, Madin and BactoTraits attestations from kg-microbe transforms" },
    { mech: "HabitatMech", dir: "in", dataExchange: true, summary: "kg-microbe supplies habitat and isolation-source data, plus terms used to identify those environments.", kind: "input", what: "GOLD, BacDive, PREGO and Madin habitat data, the environment-parameter table and the curated isolation-source table, plus ENVO / UBERON / FOODON / BTO / PO terms to ground them in" },
    { mech: "CommunityMech", dir: "in", dataExchange: true, summary: "kg-microbe helps match microbes across taxonomies and position communities on a map based on their member taxa.", kind: "input", what: "the NCBI-to-GTDB crosswalk from a kg-microbe checkout for GTDB grounding, and kg-microbe taxon embeddings for the community map" },
    { mech: "MediaIngredientMech", dir: "in", dataExchange: true, summary: "Alternative chemical names and matched chemical identities help MediaIngredientMech recognize ingredients.", kind: "input", what: "unified entity mappings and alternate labels" },
    { mech: "CultureMech", dir: "in", dataExchange: true, summary: "CultureMech can compare recipe ingredients with growth media recorded in kg-microbe.", kind: "input", what: "grounding checks against kg-microbe entities (match-kg-microbe)" },
    { mech: "AntibioticMech", dir: "out", dataExchange: false, summary: "Shared identifier namespace only.", kind: "namespace", what: "schema minted under the kg-microbe namespace (w3id.org/kg-microbe/antibioticmech); no data release into the graph yet" },
    { mech: "ProteinTraitsMech", dir: "out", dataExchange: false, summary: "Shared record formats only.", kind: "namespace", what: "shares the kg-microbe mech-shared schema modules; no data release into the graph yet" },
    { mech: "CellStructureMech", dir: "out", dataExchange: false, summary: "Shared record formats only.", kind: "namespace", what: "shares the kg-microbe mech-shared schema modules; no data release into the graph yet" },
    { mech: "NaturalProductMech", dir: "out", dataExchange: false, summary: "Shared identifier namespace only.", kind: "namespace", what: "schema minted under the kg-microbe namespace (w3id.org/kg-microbe/naturalproductmech); no data release into the graph yet" }
  ];

  // Display evidenced data relationships and complementary biological scope.
  // Preserve software/schema provenance in the source and the detailed list below.
  var graphXrefs = XREFS.filter(function (x) { return x.relationship === "data" || x.relationship === "scope"; });
  var graphHub = HUB.filter(function (h) { return h.dataExchange; });

  // Biological order also sets the ring: pathways bridge traits and cellular
  // machinery; unknown domains sit beside protein traits. Keeping the same
  // canvas and radius leaves existing nodes close to their earlier positions.
  var order = Object.keys(MECHS), N = order.length;
  order.forEach(function (m) {
    MECHS[m].name = m;
    MECHS[m].member = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(MANIFEST.mechs, m);
  });
  // Vocabularies drawn as their own chords; anything else folds into "other".
  var VOCS = ["CHEBI", "NCBITaxon", "GO", "METPO", "ENVO", "ARO", "UniProt", "DOI"];
  var VOC_LABEL = { CHEBI: "ChEBI", NCBITaxon: "NCBITaxon", GO: "GO", METPO: "METPO", ENVO: "ENVO", ARO: "ARO", UniProt: "UniProt", DOI: "citations", other: "other vocabularies" };
  var VOC_TOPICS = {
    CHEBI: "chemicals and chemical roles", CAS: "chemicals and chemical roles",
    NCBITaxon: "organisms", GTDB: "organisms",
    GO: "biological processes, protein functions or cell components", METPO: "microbial traits",
    ENVO: "environments", UBERON: "anatomical structures and host tissues", FOODON: "food materials and ingredients",
    ARO: "antimicrobial and resistance concepts", UniProt: "proteins",
    Pfam: "protein families and domains", InterPro: "protein families and domains",
    RHEA: "chemical reactions", MIBiG: "biosynthetic gene clusters", PDB: "molecular structures",
    DOI: "research sources", PMID: "research sources"
  };
  var MECH_FOCUS = {
    HabitatMech: "microbial habitats", CommunityMech: "microbial communities",
    TaxonMech: "microbe and strain identity", TraitMech: "microbial traits",
    PathwayMech: "biochemical pathways", CellStructureMech: "cellular structures",
    ProteinTraitsMech: "protein functions", DUFMech: "unknown and formerly unknown protein families",
    NaturalProductMech: "natural products", AntibioticMech: "antimicrobial activity",
    MediaIngredientMech: "media ingredients", CultureMech: "growth media"
  };
  var W = 900, H = 760, CX = 450, CY = 372, R = 262;
  var HUB_R = 46;   // declared here because the cross-reference arcs are routed before the hub is drawn
  var pos = {};
  order.forEach(function (m, i) {
    var a = -Math.PI / 2 + i * (2 * Math.PI / N);
    pos[m] = { x: CX + R * Math.cos(a), y: CY + R * Math.sin(a), a: a };
  });
  // A worklist without a published corpus has a neutral size, not a zero count.
  function radius(m) { var n = MECHS[m].records; return n === null ? 24 : 14 + 5.5 * Math.log10(Math.max(1, n)); }
  function mechURL(M) { return M.site || M.github; }
  function recordLabel(M) { return M.records === null ? M.recordLabel : fmt(M.records) + " " + M.unit; }
  function color(m) { return "var(--mech-" + MECHS[m].key + ")"; }
  // Text set in a Mech's colour uses the readable variant; marks use color().
  function inkColor(m) { return "var(--mech-" + MECHS[m].key + "-ink)"; }
  function vocColor(v) { return "var(--voc-" + (VOCS.indexOf(v) >= 0 ? v.toLowerCase() : "other") + ")"; }
  function fmt(n) { return n.toLocaleString("en-US"); }
  function esc(s) { return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) { return { "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" }[c]; }); }
  var SVG = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
  function el(tag, attrs, parent) {
    var e = document.createElementNS(SVG, tag);
    for (var k in attrs) { if (attrs[k] !== undefined && attrs[k] !== null) e.setAttribute(k, attrs[k]); }
    if (parent) parent.appendChild(e);
    return e;
  }
  // Quadratic curve between two nodes. bow > 0 pulls the control point toward the hub,
  // bow < 0 pushes it outside the ring; spread shifts it sideways so parallel chords fan apart.
  function curve(a, b, bow, spread, ra, rb) {
    var p = pos[a], q = pos[b], mx = (p.x + q.x) / 2, my = (p.y + q.y) / 2;
    var dx = q.x - p.x, dy = q.y - p.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy), nx = -dy / d, ny = dx / d;
    var cx = mx + (CX - mx) * bow + nx * spread, cy = my + (CY - my) * bow + ny * spread;
    function tow(fx, fy, tx, ty, r) { var ddx = tx - fx, ddy = ty - fy, dd = Math.hypot(ddx, ddy); return [fx + ddx / dd * r, fy + ddy / dd * r]; }
    var s = tow(p.x, p.y, cx, cy, ra || 0), e = tow(q.x, q.y, cx, cy, rb || 0);
    return "M" + s[0] + "," + s[1] + " Q" + cx + "," + cy + " " + e[0] + "," + e[1];
  }

  // Cross-reference arcs. Pulling the control point away from the chord midpoint
  // (curve() above) fails for opposite nodes, whose midpoint is the hub, and lets
  // short arcs dip under their neighbours. So an arc first tries to run outside the
  // ring: its control point sits on the bisector of the two node angles, the shorter
  // way round, and moves outward until the curve clears every other node, but no
  // further than the canvas allows. If that fails, as it always does for opposite
  // nodes, the arc bows sideways through the interior, around the hub. The second
  // arrow of a reverse pair starts one lane wider, or takes the other side inside.
  function arcPath(p, q, c, ra, rb) {
    function tow(fx, fy, tx, ty, r) { var ddx = tx - fx, ddy = ty - fy, dd = Math.hypot(ddx, ddy); return [fx + ddx / dd * r, fy + ddy / dd * r]; }
    var s0 = tow(p.x, p.y, c[0], c[1], ra), e0 = tow(q.x, q.y, c[0], c[1], rb);
    return "M" + s0[0] + "," + s0[1] + " Q" + c[0] + "," + c[1] + " " + e0[0] + "," + e0[1];
  }
  function arcClears(a, b, c, inside) {
    var p = pos[a], q = pos[b];
    for (var t = 0.04; t < 0.97; t += 0.03) {
      var u = 1 - t, x = u * u * p.x + 2 * u * t * c[0] + t * t * q.x, y = u * u * p.y + 2 * u * t * c[1] + t * t * q.y;
      if (x < 8 || x > W - 8 || y < 8 || y > H - 8) return false;
      var fromHub = Math.hypot(x - CX, y - CY);
      if (inside ? fromHub < HUB_R + 22 : fromHub < R - 30) return false;
      for (var i = 0; i < order.length; i++) {
        var o = order[i];
        if (o !== a && o !== b && Math.hypot(x - pos[o].x, y - pos[o].y) < radius(o) + 10) return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }
  function arcControl(a, b, lane, laneBase) {
    var p = pos[a], q = pos[b], diff = q.a - p.a;
    while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= 2 * Math.PI;
    while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += 2 * Math.PI;
    var mid = p.a + diff / 2, half = Math.abs(diff) / 2;
    if (Math.abs(half - Math.PI / 2) > 1e-6) {
      for (var m = laneBase; m <= 84; m += 6) {
        var rc = 2 * (R + m) - R * Math.cos(half);     // puts the curve's apex at R + m
        var c = [CX + rc * Math.cos(mid), CY + rc * Math.sin(mid)];
        if (arcClears(a, b, c, false)) return { c: c, m: m };
      }
    }
    // Interior route: bow sideways from the chord, on the lane's side, clear of the hub.
    var mx = (p.x + q.x) / 2, my = (p.y + q.y) / 2, dx = q.x - p.x, dy = q.y - p.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    // The normal is taken from the pair in a fixed order, so swapping the ends of a
    // reverse pair does not flip it and the two arrows really take opposite sides.
    var flip = a < b ? 1 : -1, nx = -dy / d * flip, ny = dx / d * flip, side = lane ? -1 : 1;
    for (var k = 140; k <= 420; k += 20) {
      for (var sgn = 0; sgn < 2; sgn++) {
        var sd = sgn ? -side : side, c2 = [mx + nx * k * sd, my + ny * k * sd];
        if (arcClears(a, b, c2, true)) return { c: c2, m: null };
      }
    }
    return { c: [mx + nx * 180 * side, my + ny * 180 * side], m: null };
  }
  var arcLanes = {};
  function arcOut(a, b, lane, ra, rb) {
    var key = [a, b].sort().join("|"), prior = arcLanes[key];
    var base = lane && prior && prior.m !== null ? prior.m + 20 : 18;
    var got = arcControl(a, b, lane, base);
    if (!lane) arcLanes[key] = got;
    return arcPath(pos[a], pos[b], got.c, ra, rb);
  }

  var svg = document.getElementById("fleet-svg");
  var gGov = document.getElementById("fleet-gov"), gEdges = document.getElementById("fleet-edges"), gXrefs = document.getElementById("fleet-xrefs"), gHubE = document.getElementById("fleet-hubedges"), gHub = document.getElementById("fleet-hub"), gNodes = document.getElementById("fleet-nodes");
  var tip = document.getElementById("fleet-tip"), detail = document.getElementById("fleet-detail");
  // Single source of truth for what is drawn and what is selected.
  var state = { layers: { vocab: false, xref: true, hub: true, gov: false }, voc: "", sel: null };

  // ---- Governance ring ----
  el("circle", { "class": "ring", cx: CX, cy: CY, r: R + 58 }, gGov);
  var rl = el("text", { "class": "ring-label", x: CX, y: CY - R - 66, "text-anchor": "middle" }, gGov);
  rl.textContent = "CULTUREBOTAI-CLAW · FLEET MANIFEST · VENDORED GOVERNANCE";
  order.forEach(function (m) {
    var p = pos[m], r1 = R + 58, r0 = radius(m) + 6;
    var t = el("line", { "class": "edge gov", x1: CX + Math.cos(p.a) * r0, y1: CY + Math.sin(p.a) * r0, x2: CX + Math.cos(p.a) * r1, y2: CY + Math.sin(p.a) * r1 }, gGov);
    if (!MECHS[m].member) { t.setAttribute("stroke-dasharray", "1 5"); t.setAttribute("opacity", ".45"); }
  });

  // ---- Shared-vocabulary chords: one per (pair, vocabulary) ----
  var pairs = DATA.vocab_edges.map(function (e) {
    var present = VOCS.filter(function (v) { return e.by[v] > 0; });
    var otherN = 0; Object.keys(e.by).forEach(function (k) { if (VOCS.indexOf(k) < 0) otherN += e.by[k]; });
    var vocs = present.map(function (v) { return { voc: v, n: e.by[v] }; });
    if (otherN) vocs.push({ voc: "other", n: otherN });
    var pair = { e: e, chords: [] };
    vocs.forEach(function (c, k) {
      var spread = (k - (vocs.length - 1) / 2) * 9;
      var d = curve(e.a, e.b, 0.55, spread);
      var path = el("path", { "class": "edge vocab", d: d, tabindex: 0, role: "button", "aria-label": e.a + " and " + e.b + ": " + chordSummary({ pair: pair, voc: c.voc }) }, gEdges);
      path.style.stroke = vocColor(c.voc); path.style.color = vocColor(c.voc);
      path.addEventListener("keydown", function (ev) { if (ev.key === "Enter" || ev.key === " ") { ev.preventDefault(); selectEdge(pair, c.voc); } });
      var hit = el("path", { "class": "edge hit", d: d }, gEdges);
      var chord = { pair: pair, voc: c.voc, n: c.n, path: path, hit: hit };
      [path, hit].forEach(function (h) {
        h.addEventListener("mousemove", function (ev) { showTip(ev, chordTip(chord)); path.classList.add("lit"); });
        h.addEventListener("mouseleave", function () { hideTip(); path.classList.remove("lit"); });
        h.addEventListener("click", function (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); selectEdge(pair, chord.voc); });
      });
      pair.chords.push(chord);
    });
    return pair;
  });
  var chords = []; pairs.forEach(function (p) { p.chords.forEach(function (c) { chords.push(c); }); });
  function chordVisible(c) { return state.layers.vocab && (!state.voc || c.voc === state.voc || (c.voc === "other" && VOCS.indexOf(state.voc) < 0 && c.pair.e.by[state.voc] > 0)); }
  function pairWeight(pair) { return state.voc ? (pair.e.by[state.voc] || 0) : pair.e.n; }
  function widthFor(n) { return n <= 0 ? 0 : Math.max(0.9, 1 + 2.3 * Math.log10(n / 4)); }

  // ---- Cross-reference arcs ----
  var xrefEls = graphXrefs.map(function (x, i) {
    var reverse = graphXrefs.some(function (y, j) { return j < i && y.from === x.to && y.to === x.from; });
    var d = arcOut(x.from, x.to, reverse ? 1 : 0, radius(x.from) + 3, radius(x.to) + 5);
    var path = el("path", { "class": "edge xref" + (x.kind === "data" ? "" : " weak"), d: d, "marker-end": "url(#fleet-arrow)", tabindex: 0, role: "button", "aria-label": x.from + " to " + x.to + ": " + x.summary }, gXrefs);
    var hit = el("path", { "class": "edge hit", d: d }, gXrefs);
    path.addEventListener("keydown", function (ev) { if (ev.key === "Enter" || ev.key === " ") { ev.preventDefault(); selectXref(rec); } });
    var rec = { x: x, path: path, hit: hit };
    [path, hit].forEach(function (h) {
      h.addEventListener("mousemove", function (ev) { showTip(ev, xrefTip(x)); path.classList.add("lit"); });
      h.addEventListener("mouseleave", function () { hideTip(); path.classList.remove("lit"); });
      h.addEventListener("click", function (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); selectXref(rec); });
    });
    return rec;
  });

  // ---- Hub and spokes ----
  var hubR = HUB_R;
  var hubEls = graphHub.map(function (h) {
    var p = pos[h.mech], dx = CX - p.x, dy = CY - p.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    var ux = dx / d, uy = dy / d, off = h.dir === "out" ? 6 : -6;
    var px = -uy * off, py = ux * off;
    var x1 = p.x + ux * (radius(h.mech) + 4) + px, y1 = p.y + uy * (radius(h.mech) + 4) + py;
    var x2 = CX - ux * (hubR + 4) + px, y2 = CY - uy * (hubR + 4) + py;
    var attrs = { "class": "edge hub " + h.dir + (h.kind === "namespace" ? " ns" : ""), "marker-end": "url(#fleet-arrow-hub)" };
    if (h.dir === "out") { attrs.x1 = x1; attrs.y1 = y1; attrs.x2 = x2; attrs.y2 = y2; } else { attrs.x1 = x2; attrs.y1 = y2; attrs.x2 = x1; attrs.y2 = y1; }
    attrs.tabindex = 0; attrs.role = "button"; attrs["aria-label"] = h.mech + (h.dir === "out" ? " to " : " from ") + "kg-microbe: " + h.summary;
    var line = el("line", attrs, gHubE);
    line.addEventListener("keydown", function (ev) { if (ev.key === "Enter" || ev.key === " ") { ev.preventDefault(); selectHub(); } });
    var hit = el("line", { "class": "edge hit", x1: x1, y1: y1, x2: x2, y2: y2 }, gHubE);
    [line, hit].forEach(function (q) {
      q.addEventListener("mousemove", function (ev) { showTip(ev, hubTip(h)); line.classList.add("lit"); });
      q.addEventListener("mouseleave", function () { hideTip(); line.classList.remove("lit"); });
      q.addEventListener("click", function (ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); selectHub(); });
    });
    return { h: h, line: line, hit: hit };
  });
  var hubLink = el("a", { "class": "node-link", href: "/kg-microbe/", "aria-label": "About kg-microbe" }, gHub);
  el("circle", { "class": "hub", cx: CX, cy: CY, r: hubR }, hubLink);
  var ht = el("text", { "class": "hub-label", x: CX, y: CY - 2, "text-anchor": "middle" }, hubLink); ht.textContent = "kg-microbe";
  var hs = el("text", { "class": "hub-sub", x: CX, y: CY + 12, "text-anchor": "middle" }, hubLink); hs.textContent = "knowledge graph";
  hubLink.addEventListener("mouseenter", selectHub);
  hubLink.addEventListener("focus", selectHub);

  // ---- Nodes: hover or focus shows detail, click opens the Mech ----
  var nodeEls = {};
  order.forEach(function (m) {
    var p = pos[m], r = radius(m), M = MECHS[m];
    var a = el("a", { "class": "node-link", href: mechURL(M), "aria-label": "Open " + m + (M.site ? "" : " on GitHub") + ", " + recordLabel(M) }, gNodes);
    var g = el("g", { "class": "node" + (M.member ? "" : " adjacent") }, a);
    el("circle", { cx: p.x, cy: p.y, r: r, fill: color(m) }, g);
    el("circle", { "class": "shade", cx: p.x, cy: p.y, r: r, fill: "url(#fleet-limb)" }, g);
    el("circle", { "class": "shade", cx: p.x, cy: p.y, r: r, fill: "url(#fleet-sheen)" }, g);
    var anchor = Math.cos(p.a) > 0.2 ? "start" : Math.cos(p.a) < -0.2 ? "end" : "middle";
    var lx = p.x + Math.cos(p.a) * (r + 10), ly = p.y + Math.sin(p.a) * (r + 10);
    var dy = Math.sin(p.a) > 0.6 ? 14 : Math.sin(p.a) < -0.6 ? -10 : 4;
    var t = el("text", { "class": "node-label", x: lx, y: ly + dy, "text-anchor": anchor }, g); t.textContent = m;
    var s = el("text", { "class": "node-sub", x: lx, y: ly + dy + 14, "text-anchor": anchor }, g); s.textContent = M.graphUnit ? fmt(M.records) + " " + M.graphUnit : recordLabel(M);
    a.addEventListener("mouseenter", function () { selectNode(m); });
    a.addEventListener("focus", function () { selectNode(m); });
    nodeEls[m] = g;
  });

  // ---- Rendering: everything derives from state ----
  function touches(sel, a, b) { return sel.type === "node" && (a === sel.m || b === sel.m); }
  function render() {
    var sel = state.sel;
    gGov.style.display = state.layers.gov ? "" : "none";
    gHubE.style.display = state.layers.hub ? "" : "none";
    chords.forEach(function (c) {
      var show = chordVisible(c);
      c.path.style.display = show ? "" : "none"; c.hit.style.display = show ? "" : "none";
      c.path.setAttribute("stroke-width", widthFor(c.n).toFixed(2));
      c.path.setAttribute("aria-label", c.pair.e.a + " and " + c.pair.e.b + ": " + chordSummary(c));
      var on = !sel || (sel.type === "edge" ? sel.pair === c.pair : sel.type === "node" ? touches(sel, c.pair.e.a, c.pair.e.b) : false);
      c.path.classList.toggle("dim", !on);
    });
    xrefEls.forEach(function (rec) {
      var show = state.layers.xref;
      rec.path.style.display = show ? "" : "none"; rec.hit.style.display = show ? "" : "none";
      var on = !sel || (sel.type === "xref" ? sel.rec === rec : sel.type === "node" ? touches(sel, rec.x.from, rec.x.to) : false);
      rec.path.classList.toggle("dim", !on);
    });
    hubEls.forEach(function (rec) {
      var on = !sel || (sel.type === "hub" ? true : sel.type === "node" ? rec.h.mech === sel.m : false);
      rec.line.classList.toggle("dim", !on);
    });
    order.forEach(function (m) {
      var on = !sel || (sel.type === "node" ? (m === sel.m || connected(sel.m, m)) : sel.type === "edge" ? (sel.pair.e.a === m || sel.pair.e.b === m) : sel.type === "xref" ? (sel.rec.x.from === m || sel.rec.x.to === m) : sel.type === "hub" ? graphHub.some(function (h) { return h.mech === m; }) : true);
      nodeEls[m].classList.toggle("dim", !on);
    });
    gHub.classList.toggle("dim", !!sel && sel.type === "node" && !graphHub.some(function (h) { return h.mech === sel.m; }));
    document.querySelectorAll(".mech-card").forEach(function (c) { c.classList.toggle("lit", !!sel && sel.type === "node" && c.dataset.mech === sel.m); });
  }
  // Two Mechs are connected if any currently drawn edge joins them.
  function connected(a, b) {
    return chords.some(function (c) { return chordVisible(c) && ((c.pair.e.a === a && c.pair.e.b === b) || (c.pair.e.a === b && c.pair.e.b === a)); }) ||
      (state.layers.xref && graphXrefs.some(function (x) { return (x.from === a && x.to === b) || (x.from === b && x.to === a); }));
  }
  function clearSelection() { state.sel = null; render(); overview(); }

  // ---- Tooltips ----
  function showTip(ev, html) {
    var stage = svg.parentNode.getBoundingClientRect();
    tip.innerHTML = html; tip.classList.add("show");
    var x = ev.clientX - stage.left + 14, y = ev.clientY - stage.top + 14;
    if (x + 310 > stage.width) x = Math.max(4, ev.clientX - stage.left - 314);
    tip.style.left = x + "px"; tip.style.top = y + "px";
  }
  function hideTip() { tip.classList.remove("show"); }
  // These descriptions name the shared biological subject, not its file format
  // or identifier syntax. Overlap alone does not establish a causal interaction.
  function chordVocabularies(c) {
    if (c.voc !== "other") return [c.voc];
    if (state.voc && VOCS.indexOf(state.voc) < 0) return [state.voc];
    return Object.keys(c.pair.e.by).filter(function (v) { return VOCS.indexOf(v) < 0; });
  }
  function joinWords(words) {
    return words.length < 3 ? words.join(" and ") : words.slice(0, -1).join(", ") + ", and " + words[words.length - 1];
  }
  function chordSummary(c) {
    var topics = [];
    chordVocabularies(c).forEach(function (v) {
      var topic = VOC_TOPICS[v] || "reference data";
      if (topics.indexOf(topic) < 0) topics.push(topic);
    });
    var e = c.pair.e;
    return "Both projects refer to the same " + joinWords(topics) + ", connecting " + MECH_FOCUS[e.a] + " with " + MECH_FOCUS[e.b] + ".";
  }
  function chordTip(c) {
    var e = c.pair.e, vocs = chordVocabularies(c);
    var examples = e.ex.filter(function (t) {
      return vocs.indexOf(t.id.split(":")[0]) >= 0 && t.label && t.label !== t.id && !/^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]*:[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$/.test(t.label);
    }).slice(0, 3).map(function (t) { return t.label; });
    return "<b>" + esc(e.a) + " ↔ " + esc(e.b) + "</b>" + esc(chordSummary(c)) +
      (examples.length ? "<br><em>Examples: " + esc(examples.join(", ")) + "</em>" : "") +
      "<br><em>Click to explore the shared records.</em>";
  }
  function xrefTip(x) {
    return "<b>" + esc(x.from) + " → " + esc(x.to) + "</b>" + esc(x.summary) +
      (x.relationship === "scope" ? "<br><em>Complementary biological coverage.</em>" : "");
  }
  function hubTip(h) {
    return "<b>" + esc(h.mech) + (h.dir === "out" ? " → kg-microbe" : " ← kg-microbe") + "</b>" + esc(h.summary);
  }

  // ---- Detail panel ----
  function linkRow(M) {
    var out = '<div class="links"><a class="go" href="' + mechURL(M) + '">Open ' + esc(M.name) + (M.site ? '' : ' on GitHub') + ' ↗</a>';
    if (M.page) out += '<a href="' + M.page + '">Page on this site</a>';
    return out + (M.site ? '<a href="' + M.github + '">GitHub</a>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function strongest() {
    var rows = pairs.map(function (p) { return { p: p, n: pairWeight(p) }; }).filter(function (r) { return r.n > 0; }).sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; }).slice(0, 5);
    if (!rows.length) return '<p>No two Mechs in the measured vocabulary census share ' + esc(state.voc) + ' identifiers in their record corpora. The heatmap shows which Mechs use this vocabulary on their own.</p>';
    return '<ul>' + rows.map(function (r) {
      var e = r.p.e, top = Object.keys(e.by).sort(function (a, b) { return e.by[b] - e.by[a]; })[0];
      return '<li><b>' + esc(e.a) + ' ↔ ' + esc(e.b) + '</b>: ' + fmt(r.n) + ' shared terms' + (state.voc ? '' : ', ' + fmt(e.by[top]) + ' of them ' + esc(top)) + (e.ex.length ? ' <em style="color:var(--muted)">(' + esc(e.ex.slice(0, 2).map(function (t) { return t.label; }).join(", ")) + ')</em>' : '') + '</li>';
    }).join("") + '</ul>';
  }
  function overview() {
    detail.innerHTML =
      '<div><p class="eyebrow">Reading the graph</p><h3 style="color:var(--ink)">' + N + ' views of one microbe</h3>' +
      '<p>Clockwise from the top the ring walks from the environment a microbe lives in to the medium it is grown in: habitat, community, taxon identity, traits, pathways, cell structures, proteins, domains of unknown function, natural products, antibiotics, ingredients, media. A culture medium is a synthetic habitat, so the ring closes.</p>' +
      '<p>Arcs outside the ring describe shared records or complementary biological coverage, dashed for linked data and dotted for complementary coverage; spokes show data exchanged with kg-microbe. Shared software and record formats are documented in the detailed cross-reference list below. Turn on the shared-vocabulary layer to see one colored chord per ontology that two measured Mechs both cite. The census of 7 October 2026 covers all twelve Mechs; TaxonMech shares taxon identifiers with most of the others. Hover a node for its detail and click it to open that Mech; click a chord, arc or spoke to see what it carries and to jump to the matching records.</p></div>' +
      '<div><p class="eyebrow">Strongest shared vocabulary' + (state.voc ? ' (' + esc(state.voc) + ')' : '') + '</p>' + strongest() + '</div>';
  }
  function selectNode(m) {
    state.sel = { type: "node", m: m }; render();
    var M = MECHS[m], rows = [];
    pairs.forEach(function (p) { var e = p.e; if (e.a === m || e.b === m) rows.push({ o: e.a === m ? e.b : e.a, n: pairWeight(p), top: Object.keys(e.by).sort(function (x, y) { return e.by[y] - e.by[x]; })[0], ex: e.ex }); });
    rows.sort(function (a, b) { return b.n - a.n; });
    var xr = graphXrefs.filter(function (x) { return x.from === m || x.to === m; });
    var hub = graphHub.filter(function (h) { return h.mech === m; });
    detail.innerHTML =
      '<div><p class="eyebrow">' + esc(M.scale) + ' · ' + (M.member ? 'in the claw fleet manifest' : 'not declared in this manifest snapshot') + '</p>' +
      '<h3 style="color:' + inkColor(m) + ';border-image:none;border-color:' + color(m) + '">' + esc(m) + '</h3><p>' + esc(M.tag) + ' ' + esc(M.extra) + '</p>' +
      '<dl class="meta"><dt>' + (M.records === null ? 'Stage' : 'Records') + '</dt><dd>' + esc(recordLabel(M)) + '</dd>' + (M.root ? '<dt>Root class</dt><dd><code>' + esc(M.root) + '</code></dd>' : '') + '<dt>Grounded in</dt><dd>' + esc(M.vocab.join(", ")) + '</dd><dt>Sources</dt><dd>' + esc(M.sources) + '</dd><dt>License</dt><dd>' + esc(M.license) + '</dd></dl>' + linkRow(M) + '</div>' +
      '<div>' + (xr.length ? '<p class="eyebrow">Cross-references</p><ul>' + xr.map(function (x) { return '<li><b>' + esc(x.from) + ' → ' + esc(x.to) + '</b>: ' + esc(x.summary) + '</li>'; }).join("") + '</ul>' : '') +
      (hub.length ? '<p class="eyebrow" style="margin-top:.8rem">kg-microbe</p><ul>' + hub.map(function (h) { return '<li>' + esc(h.summary) + '</li>'; }).join("") + '</ul>' : '') +
      '<p class="eyebrow" style="margin-top:.8rem">Shared vocabulary' + (state.voc ? ' (' + esc(state.voc) + ')' : '') + (state.layers.vocab ? '' : ' · layer off') + '</p><ul>' +
      rows.filter(function (r) { return r.n > 0; }).slice(0, 5).map(function (r) { return '<li><b>' + esc(r.o) + '</b> · ' + fmt(r.n) + ' terms, mostly ' + esc(r.top) + (r.ex.length ? ' <em style="color:var(--muted)">(' + esc(r.ex.slice(0, 2).map(function (t) { return t.label; }).join(", ")) + ')</em>' : '') + '</li>'; }).join("") + '</ul>' +
      (DATA.order.indexOf(m) < 0 ? '<p>This Mech is outside the dated vocabulary census.</p>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  var FLEET_DATA_URL = "/assets/fleet/";
  var subsetCache = {};
  var LIVE = location.hostname === "culturebotai.github.io";
  function loadFailed(extra) { return (LIVE ? 'The record list could not be loaded. Reload the page to try again.' : 'Record lists load on the live site (<a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/mechs/">culturebotai.github.io/mechs</a>); this preview cannot fetch them.') + (extra || ''); }
  function loadJSON(path) {
    if (subsetCache[path]) return subsetCache[path];
    var p = fetch(FLEET_DATA_URL + path).then(function (r) { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.status); return r.json(); });
    subsetCache[path] = p;
    p["catch"](function () { delete subsetCache[path]; });
    return p;
  }
  function recLinks(base, refs, more) {
    var out = refs.map(function (r) { return '<a href="' + esc(recordURL(base, r)) + '" title="' + esc(r[1]) + '">' + esc(r[1].length > 38 ? r[1].slice(0, 36) + "…" : r[1]) + '</a>'; }).join(", ");
    if (more > refs.length) out += ' <em>+' + fmt(more - refs.length) + ' more</em>';
    return out;
  }
  function recordURL(base, record) { return record[2] || base + record[0]; }
  // Term keys that count toward another column (build_subsets TERM_SPACE): a
  // column filter such as "PDB:" must still find PDB-CCD ligand codes (#284).
  var TERM_COLUMN = { "PDB-CCD": "PDB", "RHEA-COMP": "RHEA" };
  function termText(t) {
    var column = TERM_COLUMN[t.id.split(":")[0]];
    return (t.id + " " + t.l + (column ? " " + column + ":" : "")).toLowerCase();
  }
  function renderTermList(box, doc) {
    var q = (box.querySelector("input") || {}).value || "", shown = parseInt(box.dataset.shown || "40", 10);
    var terms = doc.terms.filter(function (t) { return !q || termText(t).indexOf(q.toLowerCase()) >= 0; });
    box.querySelector("ol").innerHTML = terms.slice(0, shown).map(function (t) {
      return '<li><b>' + esc(t.l || t.id) + '</b> <code>' + esc(t.id) + '</code><div class="in"><span>' + esc(doc.a) + ' (' + fmt(t.na) + '):</span> ' + recLinks(doc.base[doc.a], t.a, t.na) + '</div><div class="in"><span>' + esc(doc.b) + ' (' + fmt(t.nb) + '):</span> ' + recLinks(doc.base[doc.b], t.b, t.nb) + '</div></li>';
    }).join("") || '<li><em>No terms match.</em></li>';
    var more = box.querySelector(".more");
    more.hidden = terms.length <= shown; more.textContent = "Show " + Math.min(40, terms.length - shown) + " more of " + fmt(terms.length);
    box.querySelector(".count").textContent = fmt(terms.length) + " shared term" + (terms.length === 1 ? "" : "s") + (q ? " matching “" + q + "”" : "") + ", each linked to the records that carry it";
  }
  function selectEdge(pair, voc, keep) {
    state.sel = { type: "edge", pair: pair }; render();
    var e = pair.e, keys = Object.keys(e.by).sort(function (a, b) { return e.by[b] - e.by[a]; });
    var initial = keep ? keep.q : (voc && voc !== "other" ? voc + ":" : "");
    detail.innerHTML = '<div><p class="eyebrow">Shared vocabulary</p><h3 style="color:var(--ink)">' + esc(e.a) + ' ↔ ' + esc(e.b) + '</h3><p>' + fmt(e.n) + ' identical ontology identifiers occur in both record corpora, so their knowledge bases join on them without any mapping step.</p>' +
      '<div class="links"><a class="go" href="' + mechURL(MECHS[e.a]) + '">Open ' + esc(e.a) + ' ↗</a><a class="go" href="' + mechURL(MECHS[e.b]) + '">Open ' + esc(e.b) + ' ↗</a></div>' +
      '<dl class="meta" style="margin-top:.8rem">' + keys.map(function (k) { return '<dt><i class="voc-dot" style="background:' + vocColor(k) + '"></i>' + esc(k) + '</dt><dd>' + fmt(e.by[k]) + '</dd>'; }).join("") + '</dl></div>' +
      '<div class="termbox" id="fleet-termbox"><p class="eyebrow">The records behind this edge</p><p class="count">Loading the shared terms…</p></div>';
    var box = document.getElementById("fleet-termbox");
    loadJSON("edges/" + e.a + "--" + e.b + ".json").then(function (doc) {
      if (!(state.sel && state.sel.type === "edge" && state.sel.pair === pair)) return; // selection moved on
      box.innerHTML = '<p class="eyebrow">The records behind this edge</p><p class="count"></p><input type="search" placeholder="Filter terms, e.g. glucose or CHEBI:17234" aria-label="Filter shared terms"><ol></ol><button type="button" class="fleet-chip more" hidden></button>';
      box.dataset.shown = keep ? keep.shown : 40;
      var input = box.querySelector("input"); input.value = initial;
      input.addEventListener("input", function () { box.dataset.shown = 40; renderTermList(box, doc); });
      box.querySelector(".more").addEventListener("click", function () { box.dataset.shown = parseInt(box.dataset.shown, 10) + 40; renderTermList(box, doc); });
      renderTermList(box, doc);
    })["catch"](function () {
      box.querySelector(".count").innerHTML = loadFailed(e.ex.length ? ' Examples: ' + esc(e.ex.map(function (t) { return t.label + " (" + t.id + ")"; }).join(", ")) + '.' : '');
    });
  }
  function selectXref(rec) {
    state.sel = { type: "xref", rec: rec }; render();
    var x = rec.x;
    detail.innerHTML = '<div><p class="eyebrow">' + (x.relationship === 'scope' ? 'Complementary biological coverage' : 'Shared biological data') + '</p><h3 style="color:var(--ink)">' + esc(x.from) + ' → ' + esc(x.to) + '</h3><p>' + esc(x.summary) + '</p>' +
      '<div class="links"><a class="go" href="' + mechURL(MECHS[x.from]) + '">Open ' + esc(x.from) + ' ↗</a><a href="' + mechURL(MECHS[x.to]) + '">Open ' + esc(x.to) + '</a></div></div>' +
      '<div><p class="eyebrow">Evidence</p><p>' + esc(x.what) + '</p><p>' + esc(x.ev) + '</p>' + (x.url ? '<div class="links"><a href="' + x.url + '">View the source on GitHub</a></div>' : '') + '</div>';
  }
  function selectHub() {
    state.sel = { type: "hub" }; render();
    detail.innerHTML = '<div><p class="eyebrow">Hub</p><h3 style="color:var(--ink)">kg-microbe</h3><p>MediaIngredientMech provides reviewed ingredient identity mappings and an optional ingredient-context graph. The checked kg-microbe integration stages these as candidates; production promotion and a graph release require separate review and validation. CultureMech, CommunityMech and PathwayMech have exporters; their availability is separate from kg-microbe ingestion. TraitMech directs its METPO class proposals to METPO. The other listed outward ties describe shared namespaces or record formats. In the other direction, kg-microbe supplies harmonized ontologies, transformed source data, mapping tables and graph embeddings to the Mechs that need them. The exchange list records each relationship.</p><div class="links"><a class="go" href="/kg-microbe/">About kg-microbe ↗</a><a href="https://github.com/Knowledge-Graph-Hub/kg-microbe">GitHub</a><a href="https://github.com/Knowledge-Graph-Hub/kg-microbe/blob/1408e7099d039026d7611c240938d8e177753406/docs/MIM_REVIEWED_RELEASE.md#status-and-scope">Reviewed integration status</a></div></div>' +
      '<div><p class="eyebrow">Data shared</p><ul>' + graphHub.map(function (h) { return '<li><b>' + esc(h.mech) + (h.dir === "out" ? ' →' : ' ←') + '</b> ' + esc(h.summary) + '</li>'; }).join("") + '</ul><details><summary>Source and implementation details</summary><ul>' + HUB.map(function (h) { return '<li><b>' + esc(h.mech) + (h.dir === "out" ? ' →' : ' ←') + '</b> ' + esc(h.what) + '</li>'; }).join("") + '</ul></details></div>';
  }
  // Re-render whatever is selected after a filter change.
  function refreshDetail() {
    var sel = state.sel;
    if (!sel) overview();
    else if (sel.type === "node") selectNode(sel.m);
    else if (sel.type === "edge") { var old = document.getElementById("fleet-termbox"), inp = old && old.querySelector("input"); selectEdge(sel.pair, state.voc, inp ? { q: inp.value, shown: old.dataset.shown || 40 } : null); }
    else if (sel.type === "xref") selectXref(sel.rec);
    else selectHub();
  }

  // ---- Controls ----
  function syncLayerChips() { document.querySelectorAll(".fleet-chip[data-layer]").forEach(function (b) { b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(state.layers[b.dataset.layer])); }); }
  document.querySelectorAll(".fleet-chip[data-layer]").forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener("click", function () { state.layers[b.dataset.layer] = !state.layers[b.dataset.layer]; syncLayerChips(); render(); refreshDetail(); });
  });
  function setVoc(v) {
    state.voc = v;
    if (v) state.layers.vocab = true;
    syncLayerChips();
    var other = document.getElementById("fleet-voc-other"), hasChip = !!document.querySelector('.fleet-chip.voc:not(.other)[data-voc="' + v + '"]');
    document.querySelectorAll(".fleet-chip.voc:not(.other)").forEach(function (b) { b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(b.dataset.voc === v)); });
    if (other) { other.hidden = hasChip || !v; other.dataset.voc = hasChip ? "" : v; other.textContent = v + " ✕"; other.title = "Clear this filter"; }
    document.querySelectorAll("table.fleet-heat thead button").forEach(function (b) { b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(b.dataset.voc === v)); });
    document.querySelectorAll("table.fleet-heat td").forEach(function (td) { td.classList.toggle("hi", !!v && td.dataset.voc === v); });
    render(); refreshDetail();
  }
  document.querySelectorAll(".fleet-chip.voc").forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener("click", function () { setVoc(b.classList.contains("other") ? "" : (b.dataset.voc === state.voc ? "" : b.dataset.voc)); }); });
  svg.addEventListener("click", function (ev) { if (ev.target === svg) clearSelection(); });
  document.addEventListener("keydown", function (ev) { if (ev.key === "Escape" && state.sel) clearSelection(); });

  // ---- Cards ----
  document.querySelectorAll(".mech-card").forEach(function (c) {
    c.addEventListener("mouseenter", function () { selectNode(c.dataset.mech); });
    var btn = c.querySelector("[data-show]");
    if (btn) btn.addEventListener("click", function (ev) { ev.preventDefault(); selectNode(c.dataset.mech); document.getElementById("fleet-graph").scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" }); });
  });

  // ---- Heatmap ----
  var heat = document.getElementById("fleet-heat");
  if (heat) {
    var VOC = DATA.voc, max = 0;
    heat.style.setProperty("--heat-width", (184 + VOC.length * 44) + "px");
    DATA.order.forEach(function (m) { VOC.forEach(function (v) { max = Math.max(max, DATA.heat[m][v]); }); });
    var lmax = Math.log10(max);
    var html = "<thead><tr><th scope=\"col\">Mech</th>" + VOC.map(function (v) {
      var indexed = DATA.indexed_voc ? DATA.indexed_voc.indexOf(v) >= 0 : DATA.vocab_edges.some(function (edge) { return edge.by[v] > 0; });
      return '<th scope="col">' + (indexed ? '<button type="button" data-voc="' + esc(v) + '" aria-pressed="false" title="Filter the graph to ' + esc(v) + '">' + esc(v) + '</button>' : '<span title="Select a cell below to browse matching records. This vocabulary has no shared-term graph index.">' + esc(v) + '</span>') + '</th>';
    }).join("") + "</tr></thead><tbody>";
    var heatOrder = DATA.order.concat(Object.keys(MECHS).filter(function (m) { return DATA.order.indexOf(m) < 0; }));
    var vocabCounts = {}, biologicalOrder = {};
    heatOrder.forEach(function (m, i) { biologicalOrder[m] = i; vocabCounts[m] = VOC.filter(function (v) { return DATA.heat[m] && DATA.heat[m][v] > 0; }).length; });
    heatOrder.sort(function (a, b) { return vocabCounts[b] - vocabCounts[a] || biologicalOrder[a] - biologicalOrder[b]; });
    heatOrder.forEach(function (m) {
      html += '<tr data-mech="' + esc(m) + '"><th scope="row" style="--c:' + color(m) + '"><i></i><a href="' + mechURL(MECHS[m]) + '" title="Open ' + esc(m) + ' · ' + fmt(vocabCounts[m]) + ' vocabularies">' + esc(m) + "</a></th>";
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(DATA.heat, m)) {
        html += '<td class="unmeasured" colspan="' + VOC.length + '" title="' + esc(m) + ' is not included in the dated vocabulary census"><span>Not yet measured</span></td></tr>';
        return;
      }
      VOC.forEach(function (v) {
        var n = DATA.heat[m][v], l = n ? Math.round(12 + 88 * Math.log10(n) / lmax) : 0, recs = DATA.cells[m + "--" + v] || 0;
        var description = m + ' · ' + v + ': ' + fmt(n) + ' occurrences in ' + fmt(recs) + ' matching records';
        var cell = '<button type="button" data-mech="' + esc(m) + '" data-voc="' + esc(v) + '" aria-label="' + esc(description) + '" aria-controls="fleet-cell-panel" aria-pressed="false">' + (n ? short(n) : "&middot;") + "</button>";
        html += '<td data-voc="' + esc(v) + '" data-l="' + l + '" style="--l:' + l + '" title="' + esc(description) + '">' + cell + "</td>";
      });
      html += "</tr>";
    });
    heat.innerHTML = html + "</tbody>";
    heat.querySelectorAll("thead button").forEach(function (b) { b.addEventListener("click", function () { var v = b.dataset.voc; setVoc(state.voc === v ? "" : v); }); });
    var cellPanel = document.getElementById("fleet-cell-panel"), cellRequest = 0;
    function selectCell(b) {
      var m = b.dataset.mech, v = b.dataset.voc, key = m + "--" + v, request = ++cellRequest, occurrences = DATA.heat[m][v];
      heat.querySelectorAll("tbody button").forEach(function (button) { button.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(button === b)); });
      heat.querySelectorAll("tbody td").forEach(function (td) { td.classList.toggle("sel", td.contains(b)); });
      cellPanel.hidden = false;
      cellPanel.dataset.mech = m; cellPanel.dataset.voc = v;
      cellPanel.innerHTML = '<p class="eyebrow">Selected vocabulary</p><h3 style="color:' + inkColor(m) + ';border-image:none;border-color:' + color(m) + '">' + esc(m) + ' · ' + esc(v) + '</h3>' +
        '<p class="cell-count"></p><p class="cell-status" role="status" aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true"></p>' +
        '<div class="links"><a class="go" href="' + mechURL(MECHS[m]) + '">Open ' + esc(m) + ' ↗</a></div><div class="cell-records"></div>';
      var count = cellPanel.querySelector(".cell-count"), status = cellPanel.querySelector(".cell-status"), records = cellPanel.querySelector(".cell-records");
      count.textContent = fmt(occurrences) + ' identifier occurrence' + (occurrences === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' in this census.';
      if (!occurrences) {
        status.textContent = m + ' · ' + v + ': no matching records. No identifiers from this vocabulary occur in the measured record corpus.';
        return;
      }
      status.textContent = 'Loading matching records for ' + m + ' · ' + v + '…';
      records.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
      loadJSON("cells/" + key + ".json").then(function (doc) {
        if (request !== cellRequest) return;
        if (doc.mech !== m || doc.prefix !== v || doc.occurrences !== occurrences || doc.total !== DATA.cells[key] || !Array.isArray(doc.records) || !doc.records.length || doc.records.length > doc.total || typeof doc.base !== "string") throw new Error("Record list does not match the selected census cell");
        if (!doc.records.every(function (record) { return Array.isArray(record) && typeof record[0] === "string" && typeof record[1] === "string" && /^https:\/\//.test(recordURL(doc.base, record)); })) throw new Error("Invalid record link");
        count.textContent = fmt(doc.occurrences) + ' identifier occurrence' + (doc.occurrences === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' across ' + fmt(doc.total) + ' distinct matching record' + (doc.total === 1 ? '' : 's') + '. A record can contain more than one occurrence.';
        status.textContent = m + ' · ' + v + ': ' + (doc.records.length < doc.total ? 'showing the first ' + fmt(doc.records.length) + ' of ' + fmt(doc.total) + ' matching records.' : 'showing all ' + fmt(doc.total) + ' matching record' + (doc.total === 1 ? '.' : 's.'));
        records.removeAttribute("aria-busy");
        records.innerHTML = '<p>Each link opens ' + (m === "DUFMech" ? 'the DUFMech family page' : m === "CultureMech" || m === "MediaIngredientMech" ? 'the record source file on GitHub' : 'the record page or its source file') + '.</p><ol class="reclist" tabindex="0" aria-label="Matching records; scroll to browse">' + doc.records.map(function (record) { return '<li><a href="' + esc(recordURL(doc.base, record)) + '">' + esc(record[1]) + '</a></li>'; }).join("") + '</ol>';
      })["catch"](function () {
        if (request !== cellRequest) return;
        delete subsetCache["cells/" + key + ".json"];
        records.removeAttribute("aria-busy");
        status.textContent = 'The matching records for ' + m + ' · ' + v + ' could not be loaded. Try again.';
        records.innerHTML = '<button type="button" class="fleet-chip cell-retry">Try again</button>';
        records.querySelector("button").addEventListener("click", function () { b.focus({ preventScroll: true }); selectCell(b); });
      });
    }
    heat.querySelectorAll("tbody button").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () { selectCell(b); });
    });
  }
  function short(n) { return n >= 1e6 ? (n / 1e6).toFixed(1) + "M" : n >= 1e3 ? Math.round(n / 1e3) + "k" : String(n); }

  render(); overview();
  }
})();
</script>


## The twelve Mechs
{: .fleet-section-target}

Each card carries its Mech's identity color. Hover a card to trace its ties in the graph above; use "Show in graph" to select it. The small print under each headline number gives reviewed records and merged pull requests; a Mech whose measured corpus has no review-status field shows only the pull-request count, rather than a zero that would claim more than it knows. Counts were checked on October 7, 2026, with all twelve Mechs included in the vocabulary census and repository statistics. DUFMech's 6,532 seed families are labeled separately from curated mechanism records. Each Browse link is the source for its numbered card, except CultureMech, whose count comes from its <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/CultureMech/blob/26c8ba8e8d1c49ee9a118726b6facd584e7bb0fd/README.md#corpus-snapshot">repository's corpus snapshot</a>, and DUFMech, whose count comes from its linked seed snapshot. CommunityMech lists 456 communities online and keeps four additional isolate records in its repository. These published-browser totals may differ from the record-corpus census below. Fleet membership and capability declarations come from <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/culturebotai-claw/blob/73958edb97994bc16c5404443956ee64c47bfe9b/src/kg_microbe_fleet/fleet.yaml">CLAW fleet manifest at 73958ed</a>.

    <div class="num"><b>6,320</b><span>canonical media · 5 categories</span></div>
    <p class="prov">271 merged PRs</p>
    <div class="vocab"><span>ChEBI</span><span>KEGG</span><span>FOODON</span><span>UBERON</span><span>CAS</span><span>NCBITaxon</span></div>
    <div class="row"><a class="primary" href="/culturemech/">Page</a><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/">Browse</a><a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/CultureMech">GitHub</a><a href="#fleet-graph" data-show>Show in graph</a><span class="badge">in fleet manifest</span></div>
  </article>
</div>

## Explore the current sites
{: .fleet-section-target}

Alongside record browsing, the Mechs publish complementary ways to explore their data:

- **Pathway mechanisms:** browse [PathwayMech's 156 pathway summaries, labeled mechanistic edges and cited evidence](https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html). Its [repository records](https://github.com/CultureBotAI/PathwayMech/tree/ff3e716843ba8ff27970b2ba028fae328d1f9569/data/pathways) include participants, reactions and cited evidence, alongside [KGX nodes and edges](https://github.com/CultureBotAI/PathwayMech/tree/ff3e716843ba8ff27970b2ba028fae328d1f9569/output/kgx) and [SSSOM source mappings](https://github.com/CultureBotAI/PathwayMech/blob/ff3e716843ba8ff27970b2ba028fae328d1f9569/output/sssom/source_mappings.sssom.tsv).
- **Unknown protein families:** explore [DUFMech's dashboard](https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/) and its [InterPro/Pfam seed worklist](https://github.com/CultureBotAI/DUFMech/blob/f97ed39db701b1912d29b07479db65b37e15867c/data/worklists/interpro-pfam-duf-2026-10-05.tsv). Its family identifiers connect the worklist to the other Mechs through the shared-vocabulary graph and heatmap.
- **Taxa and strains:** TaxonMech lists the [20,648 taxa with a type strain](https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/type-strains.html), each naming the BacDive deposit that LPSN records as the type. Taxon pages carry strain-level NCBI, GTDB, BV-BRC/PATRIC, IMG and AllTheBacteria genome identifiers, plus StrainInfo references and their evidence. The [source catalogue](https://culturebotai.github.io/TaxonMech/pages/sources.html) describes provenance.
- **Community, trait, cell structure and media similarity:** explore [CommunityMech](https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/community_umap.html), [TraitMech](https://culturebotai.github.io/TraitMech/pages/umap.html), [cell structures](https://culturebotai.github.io/CellStructureMech/pages/embedding-map.html), [ingredients](https://culturebotai.github.io/MediaIngredientMech/ingredient_umap.html) and [culture media](https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/umap.html) through their embedding browsers.
- **Protein traits, proteins and sequences:** ProteinTraitsMech provides a [text-embedding corpus map](https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/map.html), a [map of 68,657 Swiss-Prot proteins by their traits](https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/map.html#proteins), and an [ESM-2 sequence map](https://culturebotai.github.io/proteintraitsmech/map.html#sequences) of canonical-example proteins.
- **Chemical structures:** compare compounds in the [AntibioticMech chemical map](https://culturebotai.github.io/AntibioticMech/pages/chemical-map.html) and the [NaturalProductMech structure map](https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/chemical-map.html). Both Mechs also offer a corpus map that places compounds by their record text rather than their structure: [AntibioticMech](https://culturebotai.github.io/AntibioticMech/pages/map.html) and [NaturalProductMech](https://culturebotai.github.io/NaturalProductMech/pages/map.html).
- **Habitat meaning:** HabitatMech offers a [semantic text map](https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map/) alongside its ontology-grounded record browser.

### Website features
{: .fleet-section-target}

The capability table further down lists the shared contracts each Mech declares to CLAW. This table shows what a reader can do on each Mech's own website: 24 features across the twelve published sites, with a baseline audit dated October 7, 2026. Site-specific rechecks: DUFMech (October 7, 2026). Their methods and deployed revisions are recorded below. Other sites retain the baseline audit date and method. The audit methods, feature criteria and evidence are listed below the table. A feature counts as present only if it met its criteria, so a search that lists only its first 20 matches, or cannot find record identifiers, counts as partial. Some features do not apply to every site: a page that embeds its data makes no data request that could fail, and a page with no search or filters has no search to link to. Hover a marker for its evidence, or open the list below the table. A gap marks a possible improvement to a website, not a problem with the Mech's records. The notes describe each site at its audit date, independently of the card and census pins; a later redeployment can change a verdict.

<div class="fleet-caps-key"><span><i class="e"></i>present</span><span><i class="p"></i>partial, as its note explains</span><span><i class="m"></i>missing</span><span><i class="na"></i>does not apply to this site</span></div>
<details class="fleet-site-notes">
<summary>Feature criteria and the evidence for each verdict</summary>
<h4>Baseline audit</h4><p>Each Mech&#x27;s live GitHub Pages site as it stood when checked on 2026-10-07, independently of the repository pins used by the census. An AI auditor exercised each site&#x27;s features in headless Chrome, using identifier and label searches, filters, URL restoration, page-to-page theme persistence, 390 px layout checks and deliberately aborted data requests where applicable. Changed ratings were independently re-tested; disagreements were resolved against the catalogue, then columns were compared across the twelve sites for consistent criteria. Notes state the scope of sampled pages rather than claiming an exhaustive check of every route. deployed_revision identifies a successful github-pages deployment corresponding to that site&#x27;s final browser check; several sites redeployed during the audit, and later deployments may differ. DUFMech retains the later site-specific recheck already published on main, with its own method and successful deployment recorded separately.</p>
<h4>Features</h4><dl><dt>Search</dt><dd>A labeled text search over the whole published corpus, tested with a known identifier and a known label. Present: it finds records by identifier and by label, shows how many match, and every match can be opened. Partial: results are capped with no route to the rest, no match count is shown, identifiers are not searchable, or search works only within one category.</dd><dt>Filters</dt><dd>Filter controls on record properties, with a way to clear them. Present: at least one filter on a record property in the main record listing, with a reset. Partial: filters only on a map or plot page, or a single selector without a reset. A free-text box is a search, not a filter.</dd><dt>Result counts</dt><dd>A count of matching records that updates with search and filters, distinct from the total. Partial: only a static total.</dd><dt>Sorting</dt><dd>The reader can change the sort order of a listing. Present: a sort control or sortable column headings.</dd><dt>Linkable search</dt><dd>Search and filter state is kept in the URL, so a filtered view can be bookmarked and restores on load. Partial: some state, such as a facet or a selected record, restores from the URL but the main search does not. Does not apply: the site has no search or filters.</dd><dt>Complete catalog</dt><dd>Every record the site counts is reachable from it, by listing, pagination, shards or lazy loading. Partial: some records the site counts cannot be reached from its pages.</dd><dt>Record pages</dt><dd>Each record has its own stable, linkable URL.</dd><dt>Term links</dt><dd>Ontology terms show a readable label beside the CURIE, and the CURIE links to a resolver. Present: the record&#x27;s own grounding and the terms in its main sections are labeled and linked; a few minor prefixes may be plain text. Partial: a whole main section of terms is unlinked, unlabeled or broken, or the record&#x27;s own identifier does not resolve for many records.</dd><dt>Evidence links</dt><dd>References (PMID, DOI or URL) shown on record pages are linked. Partial: references are plain text, or only a minority of the records that cite something link it.</dd><dt>Curation status</dt><dd>Each record shows its curation or review status.</dd><dt>Curation history</dt><dd>The record view shows its curation events: what changed, when and by whom.</dd><dt>Source link</dt><dd>The record view links to the record&#x27;s own source file in the GitHub repository. Partial: the file is named as plain text, or only the repository root is linked.</dd><dt>Domain visual</dt><dd>A view suited to the domain, such as a structure or embedding map, a pathway or causal diagram, an interaction network or a lineage. Partial: the visual exists but is broken for readers.</dd><dt>Cross-Mech links</dt><dd>Record views link to specific records in sibling Mechs or in kg-microbe. Partial: related records are named as plain text, or the links are broken.</dd><dt>Corpus statistics</dt><dd>The landing page shows generated corpus counts.</dd><dt>Category browsing</dt><dd>Category or class pages lead into subsets of the corpus. Partial: categories are reachable only as a filter, or their index is not linked from the landing page.</dd><dt>Suite link</dt><dd>A visible link to the X-Mech Suite directory at culturebotai.github.io/mechs/.</dd><dt>Downloads</dt><dd>A bulk machine-readable export of all records is linked from the site. Partial: only the repository, per-record files, partial exports, or a bulk file that no page links. Missing: no data or repository link at all.</dd><dt>Schema docs</dt><dd>LinkML schema documentation is published on or linked from the site. Partial: the site says a schema exists but links only the repository root, or a documentation page exists that nothing links. Does not apply: there is no schema.</dd><dt>License</dt><dd>The site states the data and code licenses, consistent with the repository&#x27;s license files. Partial: stated on only some pages, or inconsistent with the repository.</dd><dt>Build or version</dt><dd>The site states when it was built, which commit it shows, or a dated version of its own records. Partial: only the dates or releases of upstream sources.</dd><dt>Theme toggle</dt><dd>A light and dark toggle whose choice carries across pages. Partial: follows the operating system&#x27;s setting without a toggle, or some linked pages ignore the choice.</dd><dt>Narrow screens</dt><dd>No horizontal page overflow at 390 pixels on pages linked from the main navigation, the browse page or record pages; wide tables scroll in their own region. Partial: some of those pages overflow.</dd><dt>Load errors</dt><dd>When a data request fails, the page shows an error rather than an empty or misleading result. Partial: some failures are silent or misleading. Does not apply: the pages fetch no data.</dd></dl>
<h4><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">HabitatMech</a></h4><ul><li><b>Search</b> <span>partial</span>: The text map searches all 3,207 records by label and identifier (ENVO:03000033: 1 match; sediment: 138), but exposes only the first 20 result links without paging. Category search covers one category at a time. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Filters</b> <span>missing</span>: Category pages and the text map offer free-text search, but no controls for filtering by a record property. Clear filter resets text search; graph checkboxes enlarge diagrams. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/category/aquatic.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: Category search updates its count: ENVO:03000033 shows 1 of 468 aquatic records and sediment shows 89. The text map reports 138 matching records for sediment across the corpus. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/category/aquatic.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>missing</span>: Category column headings do not change the row order when clicked; the listing and text map offer no sort control. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/category/aquatic.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>missing</span>: Typing in category or map search leaves its URL unchanged. Reloading either with ?q=sediment restores an empty search. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/category/aquatic.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: The site states 3,207 records and exposes the corpus through eight categories with 16 paginated listing pages; its semantic map reports 3,207 of 3,207 input records. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Each habitat has a stable static page containing its identifier, such as marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html; category and map results link to these pages. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>partial</span>: The record label and grounding CURIE appear together, with an OBO resolver link; broader habitats have readable links. The Associated taxa section instead shows unlinked NCBITaxon identifiers. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>partial</span>: Marine sediment shows PMID:31388058 and DOI:10.1016/j.epsl.2022.117841 as plain text in graph evidence and curation notes; no PMID or DOI reference on that record is linked. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>present</span>: Record headers show SEEDED or REVIEWED; the landing reports 2,516 SEEDED and 691 REVIEWED. The site explicitly states that workflow status does not establish human review. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>present</span>: Record Curation sections show action, date, curator and rationale. Marine sediment includes ADD_CAUSAL_GRAPH (2026-09-04, codex) and SOURCE_SYNONYM_SCOPED (2026-10-07, seed_from_sources). <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: The record Provenance section links View the record directly to its YAML, such as data/habitats/aquatic/marine_sediment.yaml on GitHub. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: The marine-sediment page renders an SVG causal graph and its Show at full size checkbox works. The semantic map plots all 3,207 habitat descriptions and provides record links. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>missing</span>: Habitat record views do not expose a specific sibling-Mech or kg-microbe record link. Associated taxa remain unlinked NCBITaxon CURIEs; the footer links the kg-microbe repository. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/marine-sediment-envo-03000033.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: The landing shows 3,207 habitat records, 2,151 ontology-grounded, 176 corroborated by two or more sources and 691 with REVIEWED status, plus category and grounding summaries. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Category browsing</b> <span>present</span>: The landing and Browse page link eight categories with counts. The categories occupy 16 listing pages and provide category-wide text search. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: The shared header visibly links All Mech projects to the X-Mech Suite directory, including on the semantic-map wrapper. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>partial</span>: The site links its repository and individual YAML records, but no bulk export of complete records. The map provenance manifest is linked; the map and category JSON indexes are not complete record exports. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Schema docs</b> <span>missing</span>: The live header links the source repository, but its navigation and record pages do not link or mention LinkML schema documentation. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>License</b> <span>partial</span>: Templated pages state project data CC BY 4.0, code BSD-3-Clause and third-party terms, matching the repository. The linked standalone semantic-map page has no license statement. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map-source/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Build or version</b> <span>partial</span>: The main footer dates the upstream kg-microbe extraction to 2026-08-16, not the website build. A linked semantic-map manifest gives that projection a generated_at_utc time of 2026-10-07T18:42:23.866764+00:00. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/text-map-source/manifest.json">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Theme toggle</b> <span>partial</span>: There is no theme toggle. Templated pages follow the operating-system color preference (verified light and dark on marine sediment); the standalone map remains light. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Narrow screens</b> <span>present</span>: All 3,478 published HTML routes were checked at 390px, with no horizontal page overflow; this includes the semantic-map wrapper and standalone map, category listings, current records and retired routes. Wide tables scroll within their regions. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/habitats/aquatic-macrophyte-associated-environment-habitatmech-bacdive-38260eea00.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Load errors</b> <span>present</span>: Aborting category JSON requests makes host-associated search show filter unavailable while keeping 300 unfiltered rows visible. Static record pages and the semantic map need no data fetch to render. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/HabitatMech/pages/category/host-associated.html">Evidence</a></li></ul>
<h4><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">CommunityMech</a></h4><ul><li><b>Search</b> <span>partial</span>: Search covers names and descriptions: ANME gives 2 results and Desulfovibrio vulgaris 7, but CommunityMech:000134, NCBITaxon:2157 and the member label sulfate-reducing bacterial partners give 0. A name-search result opens its record. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Filters</b> <span>present</span>: Category, ecological state, metals, rare earth and metal relevance checkboxes combine with live counts, removable tags and Clear All. SYNTROPHY gives 32, adding STABLE gives 20, and clearing restores 456. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: The browser updates a distinct live count: Showing 2 of 456 communities for ANME, 32 for SYNTROPHY, and 20 with STABLE added. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>missing</span>: The browser has no sort selector or sortable headings; cards keep their fixed source order while search and facets change visibility. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>missing</span>: Search and filters do not change the URL. Opening browser.html?q=methane or #category=SYNTROPHY leaves the search empty and shows all 456 records. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: The browser contains 456 distinct record links, matching the 456 kb/communities YAML files at the pin. Sampled records from syntrophy, biotechnology, AMD and biomining open as full pages; four isolate files are outside the site total. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Each browser card has a stable community URL, such as communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html. Clicking its ANME search result opens the full record with taxonomy, evidence, interactions and cultivation information. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>present</span>: Sampled records link NCBITaxon, ChEBI, ENVO and other ontology identifiers to resolvers beside labels. Aalborg now distinguishes curated member/environment descriptions from explicit ontology labels; legacy MIM ingredient identifiers remain plain text. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>present</span>: Sampled community records link PMID and DOI evidence to PubMed and doi.org, with quoted snippets and support/type labels beside the assertions. ANME links PMID:37747940 and AMD links doi:10.1099/00207713-36-2-197. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>missing</span>: Sampled record headers show ecological state rather than curation or review status; evidence SUPPORT/REVIEW labels describe evidence, not the record. The community schema and records have no record-level review-status field. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>missing</span>: The sampled record template shows no curation history, including Aalborg East, although 250 of the 456 pinned community YAML files carry curation_history entries. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/Aalborg_East_Full_Scale_EBPR_Community.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>partial</span>: The footer View on GitHub link points to the repository root rather than the community’s own YAML under kb/communities/. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: Sampled AMD and ANME records draw D3 interaction networks with connected edges. The PaCMAP and graph-layout pages display 322 and 292 communities respectively, reflecting their separately generated embeddings rather than the full 456-record browser. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/AMD_Acidophile_Heterotroph_Network.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>present</span>: Community growth-media links now open specific CultureMech normalized recipe pages. Copper Biomining Heap Leach links Standard 9K Medium to pages/normalized/003277.html; the destination loads as a recipe. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/Copper_Biomining_Heap_Leach.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: The live landing tiles show 456 communities and 15 categories, matching the browser’s 456 distinct record links and the 15 categories in the pinned schema. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Category browsing</b> <span>partial</span>: Categories remain filters within the main browser rather than separate subset pages. The landing category tile links browser.html#category-facet; at 390 px the Filters button reveals those controls. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html#category-facet">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: Landing, browser, both embedding maps and the sampled record headers visibly link All Mech projects to culturebotai.github.io/mechs/. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>partial</span>: The sampled landing, browser and records link the GitHub repository but no bulk community export. The repository’s release list is empty. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Schema docs</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing GitHub card mentions the schema among repository contents but links only the repository root; sampled site navigation supplies no schema documentation or schema-file link. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>License</b> <span>missing</span>: The landing, browser, both maps and sampled record templates state no data or code license, although the repository’s LICENSE assigns CC BY 4.0 data and BSD-3-Clause code. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Build or version</b> <span>missing</span>: The sampled browser and record footers say Generated by CommunityMech without a build date, commit or dated corpus version; embedding version v3 is undated. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/communities/ANME_SRB_Marine_Methane_Seep_Consortium.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Theme toggle</b> <span>present</span>: Setting dark mode on the landing persisted via mech-theme=dark to the browser, both embedding maps and four sampled records; each rendered with data-theme=dark and the same dark background. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Narrow screens</b> <span>present</span>: Landing, browser, both embedding maps and four sampled records fit a 390 px viewport without page overflow. The Filters button exposes the mobile browser controls; wide record tables stay within scrolling regions. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Load errors</b> <span>not applicable</span>: The tested landing, browser, records and both maps make no fetch or XHR data requests. Aborting JSON requests leaves their inline data and visualizations available, so there is no separate data index to fail. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CommunityMech/browser.html">Evidence</a></li></ul>

<h4><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/index.html">PathwayMech</a></h4><ul><li><b>Search</b> <span>present</span>: Search pathway name or identifier filters all 156 records: MetaCyc:PWY-6543 and WP5587 each return 1, and trehalose returns 2; records retain their generated order. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>present</span>: Search writes q to the URL and restores it on load: browse.html?q=glycolysis opens the same one-record view with glycolysis in the search box. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html?q=glycolysis">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: All 156 record links in the browse list opened successfully in headless Chrome, matching the landing count and 156 pinned YAML records. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Every pathway has a stable records/&lt;identifier&gt;.html page linked from the browse list, including MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>partial</span>: Organism-scope taxa link to NCBI, but participant and mechanistic-edge tables still render their labeled CURIEs without resolver links; the full sweep found 27,511 table CURIE elements and no linked table CURIEs. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>present</span>: Evidence anchors lead to linked references, including source URLs for GO-CAM, WikiPathways and UniProt. Structured source assertions and exact source locations are displayed separately from quotations. Of 1,408 reference entries, 1,407 link out; the remaining CHEBI:30744 source identifier contains no PMID, DOI or URL. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/gomodel_YeastPathways_YEAST-4AMINOBUTMETAB-PWY.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>missing</span>: No record page shows a record curation or review status, and the pinned pathway YAML records carry no curation_status field. 88 YAML records additionally carry inline curation_history events. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/WikiPathways_WP478.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: All 156 record pages link Read the source YAML to the record-specific file under data/pathways/ in the GitHub repository. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/MetaCyc_PWY-6543.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>missing</span>: The record pages show biological components, source directions and mechanistic edges as tables; none of the 156 pages renders a pathway diagram, network, canvas, SVG or image. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/WikiPathways_WP5587.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>missing</span>: The full record-page sweep found no links to specific sibling Mech or kg-microbe records; no category, source-database or taxon subset index is offered. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: The top navigation on landing, browse and record pages links All Mech projects to https://culturebotai.github.io/mechs/; The landing also gives an undated KGX content hash and checksum manifest, but no build date, displayed commit or dated version of its own pathway records. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/records/gomodel_YeastPathways_YEAST-4AMINOBUTMETAB-PWY.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Theme toggle</b> <span>partial</span>: There is no theme toggle. Observed page requests were HTML and style.css; they make no data request to abort. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/PathwayMech/pages/browse.html">Evidence</a></li></ul>
