<h4><a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">DUFMech</a></h4><p>Site-specific check on October 7, 2026, <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/DUFMech/tree/1dccc4ff61998e78f12448c52f37e4d370fe7299">deployed revision 1dccc4f</a>. DUF-only recheck bound to successful github-pages deployment 6920450378 at 1dccc4ff61998e78f12448c52f37e4d370fe7299. Fresh post-129 checks verified 26 byte-exact public paths and desktop/mobile light/dark behavior, completed by a separate review of 12 new screenshots; the raw automated receipt remains unchanged. The 24 criteria also reuse retained October 7 supplementary checks of identifier/label searches, filters, counts, all sort options, copied URL state, category/navigation views, 390 px layouts, deliberate catalogue failures and JavaScript-free pagination, after all 19,881 artifact-file digest entries were verified identical. Catalogue completeness combines the exact served inventory and complete served bulk-index IDs with exhaustive offline coverage of 131 listing pages and representative public record routes, not a complete public record crawl. Deployed bytes match artifact ef7e2d01d337ce94c12abdda282a76ae5faaf313; build 3db152edc7acea72 pins corpus source 658dc8c9e4ed2587f7bf1c62ee59563f327706de, distinct from deployment. Retained external checks cover identical immutable targets and bounded mutable targets/official identity metadata; these were not all re-fetched in the post-129 run. No external provider application-rendering or scientific-review claim. GitHub code-license HTML returned 429 during the earlier supplementary check; raw project license bytes were verified separately. P33017 remains an inactive historical reference. All 6,532 families remain SEEDED and UNSCORED with zero curated functional assertions; retained reports are scoped non-scientific audits. Other sites use the fleet-wide October 7 baseline. These 24 website criteria do not promote any of the 26 repository capabilities.</p><ul><li><b>Search</b> <span>present</span>: The labelled family search covers all 6,532 families. PF04149, Domain of unknown function (DUF397) and IPR007278 each find PF04149; PF31276 is also found. Each tested result opens its family page, and browser Back restores the query. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Filters</b> <span>present</span>: Seed-status and characterization-status selects filter the main listing, with Reset. Historical DUFs give 378 of 6,532 and unknown candidates 6,154 of 6,532; combining historical status, UNSCORED and PF02698 gives one match. Reset restores all families. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: The live matching count updates with search and filters, independently of the total: PF04149 gives 1 of 6,532 families match. An unmatched query gives zero and an empty-state message; Reset restores 6,532. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>present</span>: The sort selector changes Pfam accession and protein-count order. All offered sort options were exercised and their first 50 rows compared with the complete served catalogue, including deterministic accession tie-breaking. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>present</span>: Search, seed status, characterization, sort and page are encoded in URL parameters. A copied second-page DUF search with UNKNOWN_CANDIDATE, UNSCORED and Pfam ascending restored every control and result on a new page and reload; record Back navigation also restored search. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>present</span>: All 6,532 family IDs occur exactly once across 131 static listing pages and have HTML/JSON artifacts. Completeness was checked through the verified deployment, exact served inventory, complete served bulk IDs, exhaustive offline listing coverage and representative public record routes, not a complete public record crawl. The last static page shows 32 rows without JavaScript. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Each family has its own static URL under families/, linked from the listing and paired with JSON. The verified artifact contains all 6,532 routes; sampled public PF04149, PF02698 and PF31276 pages opened successfully. This is not a complete public-route crawl. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>present</span>: Record grounding shows readable family labels beside Pfam and integrated InterPro identifiers and links to their entry pages. PF04149 and IPR007278 targets returned 200 and official metadata confirmed their identities. Provider application rendering was not re-audited. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>present</span>: Imported descriptions link their source and references. PF02698 links UniProt entries and unresolved internal InterPro PUB citations to the originating entry, not invented PMIDs. P33017 resolves as an inactive, demerged historical accession, not an active protein. These links do not establish functional characterization; the corpus has zero curated assertions. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF02698.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>present</span>: Records distinguish curation status, imported seed status and characterization status. All are scoped non-scientific audits; status stays SEEDED/UNSCORED. Records without events, such as PF31276, say none were supplied. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>present</span>: Family pages link their own data/families/PFxxxxx.yaml on GitHub at source commit 658dc8c9e4ed, plus a build-local YAML copy and per-family JSON. The sampled PF04149 pinned source and served copy have matching bytes; the source pin is distinct from the Pages deployment commit. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: PF04149 displays two frozen member sequence-domain tracks: A0A010YFW0 residues 9-60 of 67 and A0A010YYK3 residues 5-57 of 65. Geometry and labels were checked at desktop/mobile widths in both themes. These are sampled sequence coordinates, not a 3D structure, full-family architecture or alignment. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>present</span>: Related-record tables link specific ProteinTraitsMech YAML records at a pinned revision, not only the sibling homepage. PF04149 links its Pfam and InterPro domain records; the sampled Pfam target resolves and its raw record identifies PF04149/DUF397. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: Generated landing statistics show 6,532 families, 6,492 with InterPro IDs, 820 with structures and 6,478 with AlphaFold models, matching the served index. Current seed subsets are 6,154 unknown candidates and 378 historical DUFs. Upstream structure/model counters do not imply local structures or curated function. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Category browsing</b> <span>present</span>: Categories is linked from the landing navigation and opens a category index with separate paginated unknown-candidate and historical-DUF subsets, containing 6,154 and 378 families. Both subset pages opened with linked family rows. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/categories.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: A visible All Mech projects navigation link appears on the landing, category, record, sources and schema pages and targets https://culturebotai.github.io/mechs/. The suite destination returned 200 in the bounded live check. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>present</span>: Sources &amp; schema visibly links the complete index.json export. Its served bytes match the final artifact and contain all 6,532 family IDs and metadata entries with provenance. Per-family JSON/YAML and sampled member JSON/TSV are additional downloads, not substitutes for the full export. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/sources.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Schema docs</b> <span>present</span>: Sources &amp; schema links readable LinkML documentation covering FamilyRecord, the human-owned FamilyCuration overlay, event fields, provenance and enums. The documentation links the downloadable LinkML schema; both served documentation and schema bytes match the verified artifact. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/schema.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>License</b> <span>present</span>: Shared footers state project data CC BY 4.0 and code BSD 3-Clause, with license links and a third-party-terms caveat. Raw project license files matched the repository policy; the GitHub code-license HTML view was throttled with 429 during the retained October 7 supplementary check. No permission for unverified upstream datasets is implied. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/sources.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Build or version</b> <span>present</span>: Footers state site build 3db152edc7acea72 and pinned corpus source 658dc8c9e4ed, alongside dated frozen inputs. These match the served index and verified artifact ef7e2d01d337; corpus-source, artifact and build provenance are distinct from deployed revision 1dccc4ff6199. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Theme toggle</b> <span>present</span>: System/Light/Dark controls work across landing, category, record, sources, schema and cross-Mech pages. Plato checked saved preference across reload and system preference changes; supplementary navigation pages were checked in both themes at 1440 and 390 pixels. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Narrow screens</b> <span>present</span>: Main navigation pages, both seed subsets and sampled records fit 390 pixels in light and dark themes. Wide tables stay in their scroll regions; PF04149&#x27;s last related-record column remains reachable. Supplementary sources/schema/PF02698 screenshots show no incoherent overlap. This is bounded browser coverage, not rendering every record. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/families/PF04149.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Load errors</b> <span>present</span>: With catalogue.json returning 503, malformed data or an aborted request, enhanced browsing shows an error saying URL parameters were not applied, while retaining 50 static rows and static pagination. The final static page also works without JavaScript. This feature applies because enhanced browsing fetches data. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/">Evidence</a></li></ul>

Ingredient ontology query CHEBI:26710 still gives 0. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Filters</b> <span>present</span>: Checkbox facets on recipe properties combine with search and have Clear All. Category archaea gives 746 recipes; adding halo narrows to 148; clearing restores 15910. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Result counts</b> <span>present</span>: The browser shows the live matching count, including 15910 recipes, 746 recipes and 148 recipes during facet tests, and 1 recipe for CultureMech:000252. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Sorting</b> <span>missing</span>: No sort control is offered by the browser, either record index or the map data tables; sampled listings use their fixed catalogue order. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Linkable search</b> <span>missing</span>: Search and facet changes leave the URL unchanged; ?q=halophilic and #q=halophilic&amp;category=archaea both open an empty search with all 15910 recipes. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/browser.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Complete catalog</b> <span>partial</span>: The browser lists the 15910 normalized records. The landing page also counts 6320 merged records, but their pages/index.html listing is still not linked from the landing or browser navigation. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Record pages</b> <span>present</span>: Normalized and merged recipes have stable individual pages. Browser result 000252 and merged-index result media/000038 both open with HTTP 200 and show overview, composition, target organisms and provenance. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/000252.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Term links</b> <span>partial</span>: Ingredient ChEBI terms and organism NCBITaxon terms show labels and OLS links on sampled records. However the source grounding mediadive.medium:1399 on 000252 is still unlinked, now explicitly marked source link unavailable instead of linking to #. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/000252.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Evidence links</b> <span>partial</span>: References now render as links: 2asw shows both DOIs and its CCAP PDF, including growth-evidence citations. Source URLs in Notes remain plain text, such as the DSMZ Medium1399 PDF on 000252. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/000252.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation status</b> <span>missing</span>: Sampled normalized and merged record pages and browser cards still show no curation or review status. The separate growth-evidence review contains review states but does not supply a status on each record. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/000038.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Curation history</b> <span>present</span>: Sampled records show Provenance tables with timestamp, curator or script, action and notes. Record 000252 has seven events; 000038 includes the October 5 ingredient correction. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/000252.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Source link</b> <span>partial</span>: The footer still names the source file as plain text, such as data/normalized_yaml/archaea/DSMZ_1399_HALOPHILIC_MEDIUM.yaml on 000252, without a link to that file. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/000252.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Domain visual</b> <span>present</span>: The retained media PaCMAP and sfdp maps render and keyboard activation of 2asw opens normalized/000038.html with HTTP 200. Historical points without a verified current target show an explicit notice and a browser link. The ingredient map renders 653 points and a data table; Top 100 updates its visible count to 100. The old broken record Mermaid diagram is no longer rendered on 000252. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/umap.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Cross-Mech links</b> <span>present</span>: Imported recipe 015438 now links CommunityMech:000064 to its specific pinned Soybean_N_Fixation_sfSynCom.yaml source record on GitHub; the linked target returns HTTP 200. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/015438.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Corpus statistics</b> <span>present</span>: The landing page shows generated counts of 15910 normalized records, 6320 merged records and 5 populated categories, matching the pinned README and corpus inventory. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Category browsing</b> <span>partial</span>: Categories remain available through the browser facet and five headed sections on the long indexes, without category jump links or landing-page category cards. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Suite link</b> <span>present</span>: All Mech projects links to the suite directory from the landing footer, browser header, both indexes and sampled record breadcrumbs. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Downloads</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing page links the GitHub repository but offers no bulk export download. The browser loads all normalized records from app/data.js, which is not presented as a download. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Schema docs</b> <span>partial</span>: The landing GitHub card says the repository holds the schema but links only the repository root. No schema-documentation link appears in the sampled landing, browser, index or record navigation. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>License</b> <span>missing</span>: No code or data license is stated on the sampled landing, browser, map, index or record templates. The pinned repository licenses project data under CC BY 4.0 and code under BSD-3-Clause. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Build or version</b> <span>missing</span>: The sampled landing, browser and record templates give no build date or deployment revision. Dates in record provenance and the separate growth-evidence report describe curation or that report, not the site build. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/000252.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Theme toggle</b> <span>partial</span>: The dark preference persists from the landing through the browser, all three maps, both indexes and sampled records. The growth-evidence review linked from the merged index still has no toggle and remains light. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/media_growth_review.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Narrow screens</b> <span>partial</span>: At 390 px the landing, browser, all three maps, merged index and sampled records fit. The normalized index still overflows to 747 px and the growth-evidence review to 520 px; the two media maps no longer overflow. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/pages/normalized/index.html">Evidence</a></li><li><b>Load errors</b> <span>present</span>: Blocking either app/data.js or app/schema.js shows Recipes could not be loaded and Retry loading recipes. Blocking map-record-links.json shows Record links could not be loaded when a point is activated, with a route back to the browser. <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/CultureMech/app/browser.html">Evidence</a></li></ul>
</details>


## How the Mechs reference each other
{: .fleet-section-target}

Beyond shared vocabulary, Mechs name one another directly, in record fields, in schema slots, and in the curation practices they adopt from each other. The graph draws biological data links and complementary coverage; this detailed list also includes shared software and record formats. Arrows point at the Mech that consumes, or at the one a scope decision defers to. Taxa tie TaxonMech to much of the fleet through shared NCBI Taxonomy identifiers, while DUFMech connects to ProteinTraitsMech records through shared Pfam and InterPro families. Those shared identifiers appear as chords in the graph's vocabulary layer. DUFMech also retains a frozen evidence snapshot from seven sibling Mechs, with source commits and explicit link bases; those imports appear as directed data links. Direct-reference arrows require a committed record, schema or curation practice naming the other Mech.

<div class="fleet-xrefs">
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-taxonmech); --ci: var(--mech-taxonmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-pathwaymech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TaxonMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">PathwayMech</b></div><div>PathwayMech's optional CurationEvent follows TaxonMech's format; 88 pathway YAML records include 110 inline curation-history events at this pin.<small>Schema · <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/PathwayMech/blob/ff3e716843ba8ff27970b2ba028fae328d1f9569/src/pathwaymech/schema/pathwaymech.yaml#L84">PathwayMech curation_history description</a></small></div></div>

  <div style="--c: var(--mech-habitatmech); --ci: var(--mech-habitatmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-taxonmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>HabitatMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">TaxonMech</b></div><div>TaxonMech's causal-graph node types are the same 16 as HabitatMech's. A habitat node may be a HabitatMech or ENVO term, the schema declares the <code>habitatmech:</code> prefix, and TaxonMech's curation-event helper and closed-schema write gate are ported from HabitatMech's and CellStructureMech's; no TaxonMech record has a causal graph yet.<small>Schema · TaxonMech schema CausalNodeTypeEnum and prefixes; src/taxonmech/curate/curation_event.py, src/taxonmech/validation/write_validated.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-habitatmech); --ci: var(--mech-habitatmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-cellstructuremech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>HabitatMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">CellStructureMech</b></div><div>CellStructureMech's curation-event helper and closed-schema write gate are ported from HabitatMech's modules of the same names, which HabitatMech had itself ported from TraitMech. 17 of CellStructureMech's writer scripts use both.<small>Practice · CellStructureMech src/cellstructuremech/curate/curation_event.py; validation/write_validated.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-habitatmech); --ci: var(--mech-habitatmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-antibioticmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>HabitatMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">AntibioticMech</b></div><div>AntibioticMech's static-site generator is ported from HabitatMech's <code>render_pages.py</code>, including its paged category pages that filter through a JSON index, and its stylesheet is ported from HabitatMech's. Together they render AntibioticMech's whole browser. Its curation-event helper and closed-schema write gate are adapted from HabitatMech's copies; their docstrings still credit TraitMech, which HabitatMech ported them from.<small>Practice · AntibioticMech scripts/render_pages.py; src/antibioticmech/templates/style.css; src/antibioticmech/curate/curation_event.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-communitymech); --ci: var(--mech-communitymech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-traitmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CommunityMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">TraitMech</b></div><div>TraitMech's Edison deep-research runner and skill are ports of CommunityMech's <code>research_community_edison.py</code> and deep-research-community skill. Its METPO proposal skill takes CommunityMech's v1 proposal cohort as the worked example to follow, and keeps TraitMech's placeholder ID blocks clear of that cohort's.<small>Practice · TraitMech scripts/research_trait_edison.py; .claude/skills/deep-research-trait and metpo-proposal</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-communitymech); --ci: var(--mech-communitymech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CommunityMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">MediaIngredientMech</b></div><div>MediaIngredientMech imported 17 growth-media ingredient names that CommunityMech could not match to an existing ingredient. Each record keeps the name as a synonym from <code>communitymech-unmapped</code> and cites its CommunityMech source id (<code>communitymech.ingredient:name</code>). 10 records are still live; the other 7 were merged into existing records.<small>Record data · MIM data/ingredients (synonym source communitymech-unmapped)</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-communitymech); --ci: var(--mech-communitymech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-culturemech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CommunityMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">CultureMech</b></div><div>CultureMech turned CommunityMech growth media into recipes that keep their CommunityMech ids as provenance.   <div style="--c: var(--mech-taxonmech); --ci: var(--mech-taxonmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-habitatmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TaxonMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">HabitatMech</b></div><div>TaxonMech's curation rules leave a taxon's habitats and its strains' isolation sources to HabitatMech. A TaxonMech record keeps only source attestation counts, and none of its records carries habitat data.<small>Practice · TaxonMech docs/CURATION.md (record-scope table, Strains); README Known limitations</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-taxonmech); --ci: var(--mech-taxonmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-traitmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TaxonMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">TraitMech</b></div><div>TaxonMech's curation rules leave the traits and phenotypes of a taxon or strain to TraitMech, so they are never TaxonMech records. A TaxonMech record keeps only a count of the Madin and BactoTraits trait assertions about the taxon, never the trait values.<small>Practice · TaxonMech docs/CURATION.md record table; README Known limitations</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-taxonmech); --ci: var(--mech-taxonmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-culturemech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TaxonMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">CultureMech</b></div><div>TaxonMech's curation rules leave a taxon's growth media to CultureMech. A record keeps only a MediaDive count of the media linked to the taxon or to each strain, never the media themselves.<small>Practice · TaxonMech docs/CURATION.md</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-traitmech); --ci: var(--mech-traitmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-habitatmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TraitMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">HabitatMech</b></div><div>Habitat causal graphs reuse most of TraitMech's node types so the graphs stay comparable, and a trait node may be a TraitMech or METPO term (the 11 trait nodes in use are label-only so far). HabitatMech's deep-research runner deliberately follows TraitMech's conventions, and its curation-event and write-validation helpers are ported from TraitMech's.<small>Schema · HabitatMech schema CausalNodeTypeEnum; scripts/research_habitat.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-traitmech); --ci: var(--mech-traitmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-communitymech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TraitMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">CommunityMech</b></div><div>CommunityMech's <code>/ground-or-propose-metpo</code> curation command is adapted from TraitMech's command of the same name, and treats community interaction graphs as the analog of TraitMech's causal graphs. Its strict validator, writer audit and curation-event helper also name TraitMech as a source, alongside CultureMech and MediaIngredientMech.<small>Practice · CommunityMech .claude/commands/ground-or-propose-metpo.md; scripts/validate_strict.py, scripts/audit_writers.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-traitmech); --ci: var(--mech-traitmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-taxonmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TraitMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">TaxonMech</b></div><div>TaxonMech's schema lets a trait node in a causal graph be a TraitMech or METPO term and declares the <code>traitmech:</code> prefix; no TaxonMech record has a causal graph yet.<small>Schema · TaxonMech schema CausalNodeTypeEnum and prefixes</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-traitmech); --ci: var(--mech-traitmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-cellstructuremech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TraitMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">CellStructureMech</b></div><div>Structures list the phenotypes they confer as TraitMech or METPO trait ids, and a CI check tests each id and label against TraitMech's published trait index. The causal-graph node types are TraitMech's plus STRUCTURE.<small>Record data · CellStructureMech scripts/check_trait_links.py + schema associated_traits</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-traitmech); --ci: var(--mech-traitmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-proteintraitsmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TraitMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">ProteinTraitsMech</b></div><div>ProteinTraitsMech keeps a reviewed snapshot of TraitMech's TraitCategoryEnum. A CI audit that also fetches TraitMech's live schema reports a notice, never a failure, when the snapshot goes stale or when UPPER or OTHER, the only tokens both vocabularies use, is described differently (UPPER currently is). Its fallback history-record scaffolder is adapted from TraitMech's <code>new_history_record.py</code>.<small>Schema · ProteinTraitsMech conf/traitmech_category_vocabulary.yaml; scripts/audit_cross_mech_categories.py; scripts/new_history_record.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-traitmech); --ci: var(--mech-traitmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TraitMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">MediaIngredientMech</b></div><div>TraitMech leaves chemicals to MediaIngredientMech: its METPO seeder skips the material-entity subtree, which its docs say belongs in MIM rather than TraitMech.   <div style="--c: var(--mech-cellstructuremech); --ci: var(--mech-cellstructuremech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-taxonmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CellStructureMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">TaxonMech</b></div><div>TaxonMech's closed-schema write gate and its curation-history helper are ported from CellStructureMech's modules, which the code credits jointly to HabitatMech and CellStructureMech.   <div style="--c: var(--mech-cellstructuremech); --ci: var(--mech-cellstructuremech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-traitmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CellStructureMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">TraitMech</b></div><div>CellStructureMech's scope rule leaves the phenotypes a structure confers, such as motile, flagellated or Gram-negative, to TraitMech. They are never CellStructureMech records, only <code>associated_traits</code> links to TraitMech or METPO terms.<small>Practice · CellStructureMech docs/CURATION.md (record-scope table); schema description</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-cellstructuremech); --ci: var(--mech-cellstructuremech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-proteintraitsmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CellStructureMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">ProteinTraitsMech</b></div><div>CellStructureMech's scope rule makes a single protein or protein family a component of a structure, never a record of its own: it grounds to InterPro, Pfam or UniProtKB and is left to ProteinTraitsMech.   <div style="--c: var(--mech-proteintraitsmech); --ci: var(--mech-proteintraitsmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-traitmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>ProteinTraitsMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">TraitMech</b></div><div>TraitMech's <code>download.yaml</code> source catalogue follows the shape of ProteinTraitsMech's, and its <code>check_sources.py</code> validator is adapted from ProteinTraitsMech's.   <div style="--c: var(--mech-naturalproductmech); --ci: var(--mech-naturalproductmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-antibioticmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>NaturalProductMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">AntibioticMech</b></div><div>NaturalProductMech leaves antimicrobial mechanism to AntibioticMech. For the 205 compounds that share a structure with an AntibioticMech record, it links to that record and does not copy its targets, resistance determinants or MIC data.<small>Practice · NaturalProductMech schema molecular_targets; CLAUDE.md; docs/HARMONIZATION.md</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-antibioticmech); --ci: var(--mech-antibioticmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-cellstructuremech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>AntibioticMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">CellStructureMech</b></div><div>CellStructureMech's source-queue skill, which ranks the sources in its curation/source_queue.tsv, is adapted from AntibioticMech's skill and keeps its ranking rule and adoption gate.   <div style="--c: var(--mech-antibioticmech); --ci: var(--mech-antibioticmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-naturalproductmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>AntibioticMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">NaturalProductMech</b></div><div>NaturalProductMech pins AntibioticMech's structures in an InChIKey inventory.   <div style="--c: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech); --ci: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-habitatmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>MediaIngredientMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">HabitatMech</b></div><div>HabitatMech's seeder checks the label claimed by each upstream kg-microbe isolation-source mapping and, following MediaIngredientMech's SSSOM Rule B4, accepts an exact ontology synonym as well as the canonical label.   <div style="--c: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech); --ci: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-communitymech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>MediaIngredientMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">CommunityMech</b></div><div>CommunityMech growth-media components cite MIM ingredients by id (<code>media_ingredient_mech_id</code>), but they use MIM's retired <code>MediaIngredientMech:NNNNNN</code> scheme, so the ids no longer resolve. Its environment-matching tools read MIM's ingredient records and SSSOM mappings. They join on the exactMatch CHEBI term, which is what MIM's CURIE standard recommends; MIM wrote that standard for CommunityMech (MIM#119).<small>Record data · CommunityMech schema GrowthMediaComponent, src/communitymech/cross_repo_environment.py; MIM docs/CURIE_STANDARD.md</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech); --ci: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-traitmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>MediaIngredientMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">TraitMech</b></div><div>TraitMech ported its write-time validation gate and curation-event helper from CultureMech by way of MediaIngredientMech's versions, and its composite qc target mirrors MIM's.   <div style="--c: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech); --ci: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-proteintraitsmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>MediaIngredientMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">ProteinTraitsMech</b></div><div>ProteinTraitsMech's trait-merge rules, where only an exact identity match is merged and a similar name is only flagged for review, are adapted from MediaIngredientMech's same-ChEBI merge rule. Its oaklib version-pin test is ported from MediaIngredientMech's test of the same name.<small>Practice · ProteinTraitsMech .claude/skills/merge-traits/SKILL.md (just analyze-merges); tests/test_oaklib_semsql_pin.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech); --ci: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-culturemech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>MediaIngredientMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">CultureMech</b></div><div>5,700 of CultureMech's 6,320 canonical media link ingredients to their curated MIM records through <code>mediaingredientmech_chebi_term</code>, about 70% of all ingredient rows, and older records keep legacy <code>MediaIngredientMech:NNNNNN</code> ids. CultureMech also resolves ingredient labels against a pinned copy of MIM's label index, types its role slots with MIM's vendored role enums and took over stock solutions from MIM's complex-media list, while MIM's schema leaves recipe bodies to CultureMech.<small>Record data · CultureMech schema mediaingredientmech_chebi_term, mim_roles.yaml; src/culturemech/ingredients/mim_label_index.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-culturemech); --ci: var(--mech-culturemech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-communitymech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CultureMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">CommunityMech</b></div><div>Community records name the CultureMech medium they were grown in, or an environment-matched one, by <code>culturemech_id</code>, and CommunityMech's media linker copies CultureMech recipe components into them. CommunityMech also ported CultureMech's schema-validated writer and its Edison deep-research runner.<small>Record data · CommunityMech schema GrowthMedia/RelatedMedia culturemech_id; scripts/link_growth_media.py; src/communitymech/validation/write_validated.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-culturemech); --ci: var(--mech-culturemech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-traitmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CultureMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">TraitMech</b></div><div>TraitMech's curation-history helper and its schema-validated YAML writer are ports of CultureMech's, by way of MediaIngredientMech's versions, and nearly every TraitMech write script goes through them. TraitMech's qc recipe also mirrors CultureMech's.<small>Practice · TraitMech src/traitmech/curate/curation_event.py; src/traitmech/validation/write_validated.py</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-culturemech); --ci: var(--mech-culturemech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-mediaingredientmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CultureMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">MediaIngredientMech</b></div><div>MIM reads CultureMech's ingredient-occurrence table to set each record's occurrence counts, to publish a recipe-membership edge list keyed on <code>CultureMech:NNNNNN</code> ids, and to turn labels it cannot resolve into new records and synonyms. CultureMech leaves questions about an ingredient's chemical form to MIM, and MIM's validated record writer and curation-event helper are ported from CultureMech.<small>Record data · MIM mappings/culturemech_recipe_membership.tsv, scripts/refresh_occurrence_statistics.py; CultureMech docs/DATA_LAYERS.md</small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-proteintraitsmech); --ci: var(--mech-proteintraitsmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-dufmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>ProteinTraitsMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">DUFMech</b></div><div>DUFMech retains ProteinTraitsMech trait identifiers and canonical-example family classifications in its frozen cross-Mech evidence snapshot. These associations cover all 6,532 seed families; they identify reusable evidence, not validated functions.<small>Record data · <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/DUFMech/blob/f97ed39db701b1912d29b07479db65b37e15867c/data/cross_mech/cross-mech-duf-examples-2026-10-06.manifest.json">DUFMech frozen cross-Mech snapshot and source pins</a></small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-traitmech); --ci: var(--mech-traitmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-dufmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TraitMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">DUFMech</b></div><div>DUFMech imports TraitMech family mentions and Pfam links of cited UniProt proteins into its frozen cross-Mech evidence snapshot. Unresolved DUF and UPF names remain explicit follow-up candidates; a mention does not establish a protein function.<small>Record data · <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/DUFMech/blob/f97ed39db701b1912d29b07479db65b37e15867c/data/cross_mech/cross-mech-duf-examples-2026-10-06.manifest.json">DUFMech frozen cross-Mech snapshot and source pins</a></small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-cellstructuremech); --ci: var(--mech-cellstructuremech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-dufmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>CellStructureMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">DUFMech</b></div><div>DUFMech retains CellStructureMech family mentions and Pfam links of cited UniProt proteins in its frozen cross-Mech evidence snapshot. The recorded link basis distinguishes text mentions from protein-family associations.<small>Record data · <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/DUFMech/blob/f97ed39db701b1912d29b07479db65b37e15867c/data/cross_mech/cross-mech-duf-examples-2026-10-06.manifest.json">DUFMech frozen cross-Mech snapshot and source pins</a></small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-naturalproductmech); --ci: var(--mech-naturalproductmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-dufmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>NaturalProductMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">DUFMech</b></div><div>DUFMech retains Pfam associations for UniProt proteins cited by NaturalProductMech records in its frozen cross-Mech evidence snapshot. These links locate candidate biosynthesis evidence without asserting the function of a family.<small>Record data · <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/DUFMech/blob/f97ed39db701b1912d29b07479db65b37e15867c/data/cross_mech/cross-mech-duf-examples-2026-10-06.manifest.json">DUFMech frozen cross-Mech snapshot and source pins</a></small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-antibioticmech); --ci: var(--mech-antibioticmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-dufmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>AntibioticMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">DUFMech</b></div><div>DUFMech retains Pfam associations for UniProt proteins cited by AntibioticMech records in its frozen cross-Mech evidence snapshot. The associations are evidence leads, not newly validated antimicrobial mechanisms.<small>Record data · <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/DUFMech/blob/f97ed39db701b1912d29b07479db65b37e15867c/data/cross_mech/cross-mech-duf-examples-2026-10-06.manifest.json">DUFMech frozen cross-Mech snapshot and source pins</a></small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-pathwaymech); --ci: var(--mech-pathwaymech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-dufmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>PathwayMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">DUFMech</b></div><div>DUFMech retains the Pfam association of a UniProt protein cited by PathwayMech&#x27;s Mycobacterium tuberculosis trehalose-biosynthesis record in its frozen cross-Mech evidence snapshot.<small>Record data · <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/DUFMech/blob/f97ed39db701b1912d29b07479db65b37e15867c/data/cross_mech/cross-mech-duf-examples-2026-10-06.manifest.json">DUFMech frozen cross-Mech snapshot and source pins</a></small></div></div>
  <div style="--c: var(--mech-taxonmech); --ci: var(--mech-taxonmech-ink); --ci2: var(--mech-dufmech-ink)"><div class="pair"><b>TaxonMech</b><i>→</i><b class="to">DUFMech</b></div><div>DUFMech retains ten DUF/UPF-name mentions from TaxonMech records as NOT_IN_WORKLIST rows in its frozen cross-Mech evidence snapshot. They have no resolved Pfam identifier and do not establish a taxon-to-family assignment.<small>Record data · <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/DUFMech/blob/f97ed39db701b1912d29b07479db65b37e15867c/data/cross_mech/cross-mech-duf-examples-2026-10-06.manifest.json">DUFMech frozen cross-Mech snapshot and source pins</a></small></div></div>
</div>

## Orchestration: culturebotai-claw
{: .fleet-section-target}

[culturebotai-claw](https://github.com/CultureBotAI/culturebotai-claw) is the fleet's coordinator. It does not hold science of its own; it holds the definition of the fleet, the artifacts every Mech must share byte-for-byte, and tooling for cross-repository curation and validation.

<div class="fleet-orch">
  <div><h4>Fleet manifest</h4><p><code>fleet.yaml</code> is the single source of truth for which repositories form the fleet. The manifest declares twelve of the twelve projects shown here. All projects shown here have a manifest declaration. Every member declares every capability exactly once as enabled, disabled or not applicable, and every disabled or not-applicable entry gives a reason, so nothing is silently off.</p></div>
  <div><h4>Vendored governance</h4><p>Shared LinkML modules (<code>mech_shared.yaml</code>, <code>history.yaml</code>), validators and behavioral contracts live in claw and are vendored into its declared members byte-identically, pinned to one immutable claw commit and checked in CI. The canonical registry defines 20 artifacts, with each member receiving the contracts that apply to it.</p></div>
  <div><h4>Pipelines and skills</h4><p>CLAW provides pipeline discovery, repository checks, shared curation tools and validated dry runs. Agent and pipeline execution through <code>openclaw-cli</code> is not implemented; environment-curation and unified ingredient-mapping apply modes are disabled pending transactional writers. Deep-research tooling supports provider triage and dry-run result capture; provider execution is not implemented.</p></div>
</div>

The <a href="https://github.com/CultureBotAI/culturebotai-claw#current-support-status">current support matrix</a> distinguishes supported tools from planned execution. The autonomous knowledge factory label describes each Mech's curation model with human oversight; it does not imply that every CLAW workflow runs unattended.

Capability declarations from CLAW record adoption of shared contracts; they do not certify complete validation of every record field or that every failed check blocks a merge. Every disabled entry records a reason, such as no download.yaml or no source queue yet. Projects absent from that snapshot are shown as undeclared, not disabled:

## What makes a Mech
{: .fleet-section-target}

The Mechs share curation conventions, with adoption recorded per capability in the table above. The record and schema conventions below describe structured corpora; DUFMech's numeric card counts seed families rather than curated mechanism records. The website audit records DUFMech's schema documentation as <a href="https://culturebotai.github.io/DUFMech/schema.html">present</a>. The fleet standard is documented in [CLAW's MECH_STANDARD.md](https://github.com/CultureBotAI/culturebotai-claw/blob/main/docs/guides/MECH_STANDARD.md):

<ul class="fleet-standard">
  <li><b>One YAML record per entity</b>A recipe, an ingredient, a community, a taxon, a trait, a pathway, a structure, a protein trait, a natural product, an antibiotic, a habitat. The file is the unit of curation, review and history.</li>
  <li><b>A LinkML schema per Mech</b>Records validate against the Mech's schema; shared modules such as <code>mech_shared.yaml</code> are vendored where applicable. PathwayMech defines its own closed record schema.</li>
  <li><b>Ontology-grounded identity</b>Records are keyed by a public CURIE (ChEBI, GO, METPO, ENVO, NCBITaxon) where one exists, otherwise by a local identifier whose form varies by Mech.</li>
  <li><b>The ID–label invariant</b>Ontology-aware identifier–label checks compare configured fields with their source vocabulary; coverage and CI enforcement vary by corpus.</li>
  <li><b>Evidence and provenance</b>Evidence cites its source, such as a publication (PMID or DOI), a database record or a web page, as each Mech's schema allows. Mechs that adopt the source-catalogue or source-queue contract record each source's licence before ingesting it.</li>
  <li><b>Causal mechanism graphs</b>Traits, pathways, protein traits, structures, natural products, antibiotics, habitats and taxa can carry directed, evidence-backed graphs of mechanism, and community records chain their ecological interactions into causal interaction graphs. Each of these Mechs defines its own node types; most share a core set, such as chemical, pathway and biological process. DUFMech's seed worklist supplies candidate families for evidence gathering.</li>
  <li><b>Append-only curation history</b>Mechs adopting the history contract record changes as append-only events, preserving provenance across agent and human curation.</li>
  <li><b>A static browser and an open licence</b>Published-site features are recorded in the table above. The suite licensing policy is CC BY 4.0 for data and BSD-3-Clause for code. Redistributed source material retains its applicable terms.</li>
</ul>

## Related resources
{: .fleet-section-target}

- **[kg-microbe](/kg-microbe/)** - The central knowledge graph several Mechs draw from; MediaIngredientMech provides candidate ingredient mappings for it
- **[MicroGrowAgents](/microgrowagents/)** - Multi-agent media design built on kg-microbe
- **[Resources](/resources/#-ai-curation-tools)** - The full tool catalogue, including MicroMediaParam and the kg-microbe utilities
- **[METPO](https://github.com/berkeleybop/metpo)** - The Microbial Ecophysiological Trait and Phenotype Ontology that seeds TraitMech and grounds trait references across the fleet
